16	
17	# Rewrite connection platform bridge
18	
19	## 1. Scope
20	
21	本轮是东方红上位机重写的 connection real platform bridge design。
22	
23	Lane judgement:
24	
25	- Lane B design。
26	- 本轮只锁定真实 serial、TCP client、TCP server、UDP 的 platform / preload / main / renderer / connection / runtime 边界。
27	- 本轮只产出 design 和 checklist gate，不实现代码。
28	
29	Non-goals:
30	
31	- 不实现 serial、TCP client、TCP server 或 UDP。
32	- 不写 API schema、IPC channel、method、DTO、payload、错误码或枚举。
33	- 不进入 receive、send、task、SCOE、result、report 或 northbound 实现。
34	- 不把旧 `window.electron`、旧 `src/api/common`、旧 preload 聚合或旧 main business handler 迁入 `rewrite/`。
35	- 不声明 hardware、package、HTTP/FTP、northbound 或 customer validation 完成。
36	
37	## 2. Direct contract
38	
39	本设计只依据以下正式工件和当前实现事实判断范围和完成度：
40	
41	1. `AGENTS.md`
42	2. `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-execution-charter.md`
43	3. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
44	4. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md`
45	5. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-platform-api-surface-reduction.md`
46	6. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-transport-boundary.md`
47	7. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-platform-app-shell-file-dialog.md`
48	8. `codestable/quality/rewrite-validation-fixture-oracle-baseline.md`
49	9. `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
50	10. `codestable/quality/rewrite-review-checklist.md`
51	11. `codestable/features/rewrite-connection/rewrite-connection-design.md`
52	12. `codestable/features/rewrite-connection/rewrite-connection-checklist.yaml`
53	13. 当前实现：`rewrite/src/features/connection`
54	14. 当前实现：`rewrite/src/platform`
55	15. 当前实现：`rewrite/src-electron/main`
56	16. 当前实现：`rewrite/src-electron/preload`
57	
58	Boundary guards:
59	
60	- 只设计真实 serial / TCP client / TCP server / UDP bridge 的职责边界。
61	- 不冻结 schema、channel、method、DTO、payload、错误码或枚举。
62	- renderer 只能通过 `rewrite/src/platform` facade 使用桌面能力。
63	- preload 只暴露 typed bridge，不暴露裸 `invoke/send/on` 或任意 channel 订阅。
64	- main 只承载 native resource、transport lifecycle、OS I/O、必要 buffering、batch、queue、throttle 和 backpressure。
65	- main 禁止解释 frame、receive、send、task、SCOE、result、report、northbound 业务语义。
66	- connection feature 消费 platform capability，但不绑定具体 platform schema。
67	- 旧 serial/network target 不是 northbound `deviceId`，也不是业务设备、task、case 或 report delivery target。
68	- 不读取或修改自动生成前端 types。
69	- 本轮纯文档，不运行 build/lint。
70	
71	## 3. Current evidence
72	
73	当前 `rewrite` 状态：
74	
75	- `rewrite/src/shared/platform-bridge.ts` 只定义 bridge metadata，capability 类型当前为空。
76	- `rewrite/src/platform/index.ts` 只读取 `getBridgeInfo()`，没有真实 transport facade。
77	- `rewrite/src-electron/preload/index.ts` 只通过 `contextBridge` 暴露 metadata-only typed bridge。
78	- `rewrite/src-electron/main/index.ts` 只创建窗口并配置安全边界，没有 transport IPC handler。
79	- `rewrite/src/features/connection` 已有 core、state、selector、service、adapter port 和 fake adapter，当前真实平台能力尚未接入。
80	- 当前 connection 测试以 fake adapter、pure core、state/service/selector 为主，不能证明真实串口、TCP 或 UDP 链路。
81	
82	旧系统可作为 evidence 的 transport 能力：
83	
84	- serial 侧存在端口枚举、打开、关闭、关闭全部、读写、状态、事件广播和 app quit cleanup。
85	- TCP client 侧存在 socket connect、send、receive、close、error、timeout。
86	- TCP server 侧存在 listen、client accept、client list、broadcast write 和 cleanup。
87	- UDP 侧存在 bind、message、send、remote host route 和 broadcast candidate。
88	- 旧 renderer 通过 `window.electron` 和 `src/api/common` 使用 serial/network 能力，旧 main/preload/renderer 链路提供了能力清单 evidence，但不能作为目标 bridge 形状。
89	
90	旧系统风险 evidence：
91	
92	- 旧 preload 暴露大而全 `window.electron`。
93	- 旧 renderer wrapper 围绕 `src/api/common/*Api.ts` 复制 Electron 能力面。
94	- 旧 serial/network data 逐事件进入 renderer store，并继续推动 receive、send、task、SCOE、统计和 UI 副作用。
95	- 旧 network handler 中存在 TCP `\\r\\n` 分包、高速存储 short-circuit 和全窗口广播。
96	- 旧 main 中存在 receive business handler，说明旧主进程已混入业务语义，不能成为新职责依据。
97	
98	Evidence judgement:
99	
100	- 旧 transport 能力可作为 native resource 能力范围和 manual/hardware oracle 候选。
101	- 旧暴露方式、旧 store 副作用链、旧 main business handler、旧 target 字符串和旧高速短路规则只作为迁移风险，不进入目标平台桥接合同。
102	
103	## 4. Owner split
104	
105	| Owner | Owns | Must not own |
106	| --- | --- | --- |
107	| `platform` | renderer 侧 facade；桥接可用性、capability health、受控 transport primitive category；面向 connection real adapter 的唯一桌面能力入口。 | 连接配置语义、target identity 语义、receive parsing、send result、task lifecycle、SCOE protocol、result/report/northbound 语义；旧 `src/api/common` 一一平移。 |
108	| `preload` | 最小 typed bridge；把 platform facade 允许的 transport capability 接到 main；订阅清理和 renderer 隔离边界。 | 裸 `invoke/send/on`、任意 channel 订阅、`window.electron` 大包、receive/send/task/SCOE/report/northbound business API。 |
109	| `main` | serial port、TCP socket、TCP server、UDP socket 等 native resource handle；资源生命周期；OS I/O；必要 buffer、batch、queue、throttle、backpressure、cleanup。 | frame matching、receive classification、send task state、SCOE completion、result attribution、report generation、northbound error conversion、customer device identity。 |
110	| `renderer` | 页面、feature 和 runtime 通过 `rewrite/src/platform` facade 与 connection public boundary 使用能力；只消费只读 snapshot、public service 或 runtime route。 | Node/Electron/`ipcRenderer`/`fs`/`path`/`net`/`serialport` 直连；直接订阅 raw preload event；直接持有 socket/serialport/platform handles。 |
111	| `connection` | transport config meaning、runtime connection facts、target availability、transport lifecycle、transport-level event/error/read model；real/fake adapter port。 | API channel/schema 定义权、业务帧解析、send 构帧、task 推进、SCOE 语义、result/report/northbound 语义、platform resource handle。 |
112	| `runtime` | app 级装配、connection service/adapter/subscription 创建和释放、跨 feature 路由、app shutdown cleanup、boundary exception registry。 | transport native resource 细节、receive parser、send builder、task lifecycle、SCOE protocol、report/northbound 语义、隐藏全局 event bus。 |
113	
114	Key split:
115	
116	- main owns live resource facts needed to operate handles.
117	- connection owns canonical renderer-side connection facts.
118	- runtime owns wiring and exception visibility.
119	- platform/preload own the bridge boundary, not business interpretation.
120	
121	## 5. Allowed capability categories
122	
123	本文只允许能力类别，不定义具体接口形状。
124	
125	| Category | Allowed meaning | Consumer |
126	| --- | --- | --- |
127	| Bridge health metadata | bridge 是否存在、版本/能力类别健康、transport bridge 是否可用。 | app/platform/runtime |
128	| Serial discovery | 枚举可见 serial resource 候选和基础技术可用性。 | connection adapter |
129	| Transport lifecycle | serial open/close/cleanup、TCP client connect/close、TCP server listen/close/client cleanup、UDP bind/close。 | connection adapter |
130	| Byte input | serial/TCP/UDP 入站 bytes 的受控 batch 或 normalized transport input event。 | connection -> runtime -> receive |
131	| Byte output | transport-level bytes write request 和技术接收/失败材料。 | send/SCOE -> runtime/connection |
132	| Transport resource facts | connected、closed、resource unavailable、client accepted、remote route visible 等 transport facts。 | connection read model |
133	| Transport errors | open/connect/listen/bind/write/timeout/close/cleanup 等 transport-level error category。 | connection read model, status/pages |
134	| Subscription lifecycle | 订阅建立、释放、stale event 处理、app shutdown cleanup。 | runtime/platform/preload/main |
135	| Buffer/backpressure | 与 native resource 或性能压力绑定的 batch、queue、throttle、overflow/backpressure signal。 | main/platform/connection/runtime |
136	| Fake/real adapter substitution | fake adapter 继续用于 Vitest/fixture，real adapter 通过 platform facade 接入。 | connection tests/runtime |
137	
138	Transport-specific allowance:
139	
140	- Serial bridge 只作为 native port resource lifecycle 和 byte I/O。
141	- TCP client bridge 只作为 socket lifecycle 和 byte I/O。
142	- TCP server bridge 只作为 listener/client socket lifecycle 和 byte I/O。
143	- UDP bridge 只作为 socket bind、message、send、remote route transport material。
144	
145	这些类别不授权 platform/main 定义连接配置 schema、target id 形状、receive 输入 schema、send result、task result、SCOE completion 或 northbound identity。
146	
147	## 6. Forbidden surfaces
148	
149	Forbidden:
150	
151	- 禁止重新暴露 `window.electron` 大包。
152	- 禁止 renderer 可见裸 `invoke/send/on` 或任意 channel 订阅。
153	- 禁止 renderer 直接访问 Node、Electron、`ipcRenderer`、`fs`、`path`、`net`、`serialport`。
154	- 禁止 page、widget、feature 直接访问 preload bridge 细节或 main channel。
155	- 禁止 platform facade 复制旧 `src/api/common/serialApi.ts`、`networkApi.ts` 或旧 preload 文件形状。
156	- 禁止 main 解析 frame、匹配 receive、构造 send result、推进 task、解释 SCOE、生成 report 或转换 northbound error。
157	- 禁止 main 用 high-speed storage rule、receive rule 或 SCOE rule 决定 bytes 是否进入业务链路，除非有正式 runtime exception，且 business owner 和 exit condition 清楚。
158	- 禁止全窗口无差别广播 raw high-frequency bytes 作为目标设计。
159	- 禁止 connection public boundary 暴露 mutable state、store、socket、serialport、Electron object、raw channel 或 platform adapter。
160	- 禁止把 serial/network transport target 直接映射为 northbound `deviceId`、customer device 或 report delivery target。
161	
162	Review stance:
163	
164	- 重新引入上述 surface 的实现应判为 `revise-required`。
165	- 涉及真实 platform bridge 但未把本文列为 direct contract 或 boundary guard 的实现审查应判为 `blocked`。
166	
167	## 7. Data flow boundary
168	
169	Target flow:
170	
171	```text
172	native resource in main
173	  -> resource lifecycle / byte I/O
174	  -> main-side buffer / batch / queue / backpressure when justified
175	  -> minimal typed preload bridge
176	  -> rewrite/src/platform facade
177	  -> connection real transport adapter
178	  -> connection service/state/read model
179	  -> runtime route or explicit feature event
180	  -> receive / send / SCOE / status public boundary
181	  -> feature-owned read model
182	  -> throttled UI snapshot
183	```
184	
185	Rules:
186	
187	- Low-frequency lifecycle/error facts and high-frequency byte input must use separate design lanes.
188	- Byte input reaches receive as transport bytes/batches plus source material. connection does not parse it.
189	- Byte output reaches main as transport write material. send/SCOE own business request and business result.
190	- Status may read connection summary, but does not own connection truth.
191	- Runtime may route bytes and writes, but does not inspect protocol semantics.
192	- UI consumes snapshots and commands, not raw transport events.
193	
194	## 8. Event, buffer, and backpressure principles
195	
196	Main may retain only these platform-side or performance-side processing categories:
197	
198	- input byte batching for serial/TCP/UDP data.
199	- write queueing tied to a live native resource.
200	- subscription queueing and cleanup to prevent stale event delivery.
201	- bounded lifecycle/error event buffering for renderer synchronization.
202	- throttle or coalesce for high-frequency telemetry and UI-safe event delivery.
203	- overflow/backpressure signal when queue/batch policy is exceeded.
204	- resource cleanup drain on close, app shutdown or stale connection generation.
205	
206	Rules:
207	
208	- Queue depth、batch window、overflow/drop policy、sampling、UI refresh interval and target throughput are deferred to implementation/runtime evidence.
209	- Overflow must be observable as transport-level material, not silently converted into receive/send/task/result failure.
210	- Main may compress or batch events for transport pressure, but may not classify bytes as frame match、task trigger、SCOE success、report material or northbound delivery status.
211	- connection may maintain transport counters and recent event snapshots, but business statistics belong to receive/send/task/SCOE/status/result owners.
212	- Runtime exception records are required for high-frequency buffer/backpressure, high-speed storage short-circuit, packaged native module behavior and SCOE fixed route.
213	- Development-time fake adapter tests can simulate backpressure categories, but real steady-state behavior requires runtime/hardware validation after parameters are designed.
214	
215	## 9. Error and lifecycle boundary
216	
217	Principles:
218	
219	- Main emits raw resource outcomes and technical failures.
220	- Platform/preload carry typed technical results without exposing raw IPC or raw handles.
221	- Connection normalizes transport-level lifecycle and error facts.
222	- Consumers interpret those facts under their own feature designs.
223	
224	Lifecycle boundaries:
225	
226	- connect、disconnect、cleanup and app shutdown must be idempotent enough for duplicate user actions, stale events and already-closed resources.
227	- Automatic reconnect is not assumed. If introduced, retry policy、cancellation、UI visibility and runtime/hardware validation must be declared separately.
228	- TCP server client lifecycle is transport availability material, not a business session.
229	- UDP remote route is transport route material, not a customer device identity.
230	
231	Error boundaries:
232	
233	- A transport write failure is input material for send/SCOE; it does not define task lifecycle.
234	- A disconnect or timeout is a connection fact; it does not define result/report/northbound failure.
235	- Missing resource, port unavailable, socket error or cleanup failure can be normalized by connection, but this document does not freeze error code names.
236	- User-visible error text belongs to pages/status projection and must not leak raw platform internals.
237	
238	## 10. Connection consumption posture
239	
240	Current connection fake loop remains valid as the first adapter contract layer:
241	
242	- core stays pure TypeScript.
243	- service depends on adapter port.
244	- state/selectors expose immutable read models.
245	- fake adapter remains the fixture/Vitest implementation.
246	
247	Future real adapter gate:
248	
249	- real adapter may call only `rewrite/src/platform` transport capability categories accepted by an implementation design.
250	- real adapter must map platform technical material into existing connection service inputs without exposing platform resource handles.
251	- connection may consume discovery, lifecycle, byte input/output, transport error and backpressure material.
252	- connection must not bind to final preload/main schema in this architecture document.
253	- connection must not expose real adapter or platform facade to receive/send/task/SCOE/pages.
254	
255	Cross-feature consumption:
256	
257	- receive consumes byte batches and source material through runtime or explicit event.
258	- send/SCOE consume transport write capability and transport-level write outcome through public boundary.
259	- task consumes transport availability or send failure material only through task/send design.
260	- status consumes read-only connection summary.
261	- northbound has no direct serial/TCP/UDP target consumption in this design.
262	
263	## 11. Validation plan
264	
265	| Layer | Can prove | Cannot prove |
266	| --- | --- | --- |
267	| Static scan | 文档章节、direct contract、boundary guards、禁止面、renderer 无 raw Electron/Node 入口、platform 不复刻旧 API。 | 真实 I/O、event order、性能、硬件、打包态、客户闭环。 |
268	| Vitest unit | connection core lifecycle、config validation category、selector、state transition、error/event normalization。 | Electron runtime、preload/main、真实 serial/TCP/UDP。 |
269	| Fixture test | fake serial/TCP/TCP server/UDP event、target availability、stale event、write failure、cleanup、overflow simulation after policy exists。 | OS driver、socket timing、真实吞吐、真实断线。 |
270	| Fake adapter test | service/adapter contract under success、failure、duplicate close、disconnect during write、stale event。 | main/preload/platform bridge correctness。 |
271	| Runtime validation | Electron app startup/shutdown、bridge subscription cleanup、event ordering、fake/real adapter wiring、raw event storm replacement。 | 真实硬件链路、打包态 native module、客户交付。 |
272	| Package validation | packaged bridge loading、native module loading、resource path、extraResources、packaged runtime differences if touched。 | 业务算法、真实硬件时序、northbound customer closure。 |
273	| Hardware validation | real serial enumerate/open/close/reopen/send/receive/error/unplug；real TCP client/server/UDP connect/send/receive/close/error/timeout；high-frequency behavior after parameters are designed。 | package path、customer schema、HTTP/FTP delivery closure。 |
274	| Customer validation | 本设计不适用，只作为 blocker guard。 | 不能用 connection target 推导 northbound identity 或 customer acceptance。 |
275	
276	Cannot claim from this design:
277	
278	- real serial/TCP/UDP hardware chain complete.
279	- high-frequency steady-state complete.
280	- packaged transport bridge/native module behavior complete.
281	- SCOE fixed source/target complete.
282	- HTTP/FTP/northbound delivery complete.
283	- customer closure complete.
284	
285	## 12. Implementation gate
286	
287	进入真实 bridge implementation 前必须满足：
288	
289	1. 实现轮重新列出 Direct contract / Boundary guards，并把本文列入 direct contract 或 boundary guard。
290	2. 明确本轮仅接受哪些 capability categories，不写超出当前轮的 schema、channel、DTO、payload 或错误码。
291	3. 明确 owner split：platform、preload、main、renderer、connection、runtime 各自改什么、不改什么。
292	4. 明确 real adapter 如何继续遵守 `ConnectionTransportAdapter` port，不暴露 platform/main resource handle。
293	5. 明确 lifecycle/error/event 的 source、normalization owner、consumer 和 cleanup 责任。
294	6. 明确 high-frequency queue/batch/backpressure 的设计状态：已设计、deferred 或 blocker。
295	7. 如 main 保留 buffer/backpressure、stream/file path、native module 或 package-sensitive 行为，必须登记 runtime boundary exception。
296	8. 明确 validation levels：Vitest/fake 能覆盖什么，runtime/package/hardware 必须验证什么。
297	9. 静态扫描确认 renderer 不直接访问 Node/Electron/raw IPC，preload 不暴露裸通道，main 不承载业务语义。
298	10. 未完成 runtime/package/hardware evidence 前，不声明真实连接链路验收完成。
299	
300	不能进入 implementation 的情况：
301	
302	- 需要 final API schema 才能判断边界。
303	- 需要 receive/send/task/SCOE/result/report/northbound 语义才能写 transport bridge。
304	- 需要 customer `deviceId` 或 northbound schema 才能解释 serial/network target。
305	- 缺少 queue/backpressure 策略却要声明 high-frequency steady-state accepted。
306	- 缺少 package/native module 验证计划却要触达 packaged bridge loading。
307	
308	## 13. Deferred and blockers
309	
310	Deferred:
311	
312	- Concrete platform/preload/main/renderer schema、channel、method、DTO、payload、error code、enum。
313	- Concrete connection config model、target id representation、persistence shape and migration strategy。
314	- Queue depth、batch window、overflow/drop policy、sampling、UI refresh interval and target throughput。
315	- TCP `\\r\\n` split ownership: transport input normalization、receive compatibility rule or candidate drop。
316	- High-speed storage short-circuit boundary with storage/receive/runtime。
317	- Global `webContents` broadcast replacement and target window/subscriber scoping。
318	- Network status-change drift between old preload listener and main event evidence。
319	- Serial option update while connected and whether close/reopen remains visible behavior。
320	- UDP remote route representation, including IPv6 and duplicate route behavior。
321	- SCOE fixed TCP server / UDP source and target exception。
322	- Northbound `deviceId` and transport target separation in customer-facing flows。
323	
324	Blockers for implementation:
325	
326	- No accepted implementation design for concrete platform transport bridge capability categories.
327	- No high-frequency queue/batch/backpressure policy for claims beyond basic lifecycle.
328	- No package/native-module validation plan if implementation touches packaged bridge loading or native resources.
329	- No runtime plan for subscription cleanup, stale event handling and app shutdown cleanup.
330	
331	Blockers for acceptance:
332	
333	- Missing real serial/TCP/UDP validation blocks claiming hardware connection accepted.
334	- Missing runtime high-frequency evidence blocks claiming batching/backpressure accepted.
335	- Missing package validation blocks claiming packaged transport bridge/native module behavior accepted.
336	- Missing SCOE device evidence blocks claiming SCOE fixed source/target accepted.
337	- Missing customer schema/environment blocks claiming northbound delivery accepted.
338	
339	## 14. Readiness verdict
340	
341	`ready-for-implementation-design`, not ready for implementation.
342	
343	Reason:
344	
345	- Boundary owner split is now explicit.
346	- Current rewrite implementation has fake connection contracts but no real platform bridge.
347	- Real bridge implementation still needs a concrete implementation design for accepted capability categories, event model, queue/backpressure policy, runtime/package/hardware validation plan and exception registry.
348	
```

17	
18	# Rewrite connection bridge implementation design
19	
20	## 1. Direct contract
21	
22	本设计只依据以下正式工件和当前实现事实判断范围和完成度：
23	
24	1. `AGENTS.md`
25	2. `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-execution-charter.md`
26	3. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
27	4. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md`
28	5. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-platform-api-surface-reduction.md`
29	6. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-transport-boundary.md`
30	7. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md`
31	8. `codestable/quality/rewrite-validation-fixture-oracle-baseline.md`
32	9. `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
33	10. `codestable/quality/rewrite-review-checklist.md`
34	11. `codestable/features/rewrite-connection/rewrite-connection-design.md`
35	12. `codestable/features/rewrite-connection/rewrite-connection-checklist.yaml`
36	13. 当前实现：`rewrite/src/features/connection`（core、services、state、adapters）
37	14. 当前实现：`rewrite/src/platform`（facade stub）
38	15. 当前实现：`rewrite/src-electron/main`（bare window manager）
39	16. 当前实现：`rewrite/src-electron/preload`（metadata-only bridge）
40	
41	Boundary guards：
42	
43	- 本轮是 Lane B implementation design，只产出 design + checklist 更新，不实现代码。
44	- 不实现 serial、TCP client、TCP server 或 UDP 真实平台链路。
45	- 不冻结 channel、method、DTO、字段、错误码或枚举。
46	- 不进入 receive、send、task、SCOE、result、report、northbound 实现。
47	- main 只承载平台 transport 资源、生命周期和必要 buffer/batch/queue/backpressure，不解释业务语义。
48	- renderer 不直接访问 Node、Electron、`ipcRenderer`、`fs`、`path`、`net`、`serialport` 或旧 `window.electron`。
49	- serial/network transport target 不等于 northbound `deviceId`、业务设备身份、task、case 或 report delivery target。
50	- 不声明 hardware、package、HTTP/FTP、northbound 或 customer validation 完成。
51	- 旧 `src/`、`src-electron/`、`public/` 只作为 evidence、fixture 或 oracle 输入。
52	- 不读取或修改前端自动生成 types 文件。
53	
54	## 2. Current implementation evidence
55	
56	### 2.1 Connection adapter port
57	
58	当前 `ConnectionTransportAdapter` 接口（`rewrite/src/features/connection/adapters/ports.ts`）定义：
59	
60	| Method | Signature | Purpose |
61	| --- | --- | --- |
62	| `connect` | `(config: TransportConfig) => Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>` | 建立 transport 连接 |
63	| `disconnect` | `(connectionId: string) => Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>` | 断开指定连接 |
64	| `write` | `(request: TransportWriteRequest) => Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>` | 发送 bytes |
65	| `cleanup` | `() => Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>` | 释放所有连接 |
66	| `drainEvents` | `() => Promise<readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[]>` | 拉取异步事件队列 |
67	
68	`ConnectionAdapterEvent` 定义了 6 种事件类别：`connected`、`disconnected`、`data`、`write-accepted`、`write-failed`、`error`。
69	
70	`TransportWriteRequest` 携带 `connectionId`、`bytes` 和可选 `targetId`。
71	
72	`ConnectionResourceCandidate` 定义 discovery 输出：`kind`、`id`、`label`。
73	
74	Judgement：该 port 接口已覆盖基本生命周期和字节 I/O，但缺少 discovery（枚举串口、发现网络资源）和 backpressure signal。real adapter 需要在遵守该 port 的同时通过 platform facade 补充 discovery 能力。
75	
76	### 2.2 Fake adapter
77	
78	`FakeConnectionTransportAdapter`（`fake-transport-adapter.ts`）提供：
79	
80	- 成功和失败注入（`setFailure`、`clearFailures`）
81	- 外部事件推送（`pushData`、`pushError`、`pushClose`）
82	- 状态观察（`readStoredConfig`、`readWrittenBatches`）
83	- 所有 4 种 transport kind 的 config 接受和 lifecycle 模拟
84	
85	Gap：fake adapter 目前不模拟 backpressure、discovery、TCP server 多 client 或 UDP remote route 变化。real adapter 到来前不需要改 fake adapter，但后续应补齐 backpressure 和 discovery 场景。
86	
87	### 2.3 Platform/preload/main scaffold
88	
89	| Layer | Current state | Gap for real bridge |
90	| --- | --- | --- |
91	| `platform-bridge.ts` | `RewritePlatformCapability = never`，只有 `getBridgeInfo()` | 需要定义 transport capability type |
92	| `platform/index.ts` | 只导出 `getRewritePlatformBridgeInfo()` | 需要 transport facade 方法 |
93	| `preload/index.ts` | 只暴露 `getBridgeInfo()` via contextBridge | 需要 transport IPC bridge |
94	| `main/index.ts` | bare BrowserWindow，无 IPC handler | 需要 transport resource handler |
95	
96	### 2.4 Old implementation risk summary
97	
98	基于子 agent 调查的旧代码证据：
99	
100	| 风险类别 | 旧代码证据 | 严重程度 |
101	| --- | --- | --- |
102	| TCP `\r\n` 分包无跨 chunk 缓冲 | `networkHandlers.ts:466-493`，跨 segment 边界的 `\r\n` 丢失 | CRITICAL |
103	| 高速存储业务逻辑嵌入 transport handler | `networkHandlers.ts:509-516`，`shouldStore` 判断后短路 renderer | HIGH |
104	| 全窗口无差别广播 raw data | `serialHandlers.ts:532`、`networkHandlers.ts:598-608`，`getAllWindows()/getAllWebContents()` | HIGH |
105	| TCP server 不识别独立 client | `networkHandlers.ts:146-224`，client 按 index 而非稳定 ID | HIGH |
106	| 串口无 batching/throttling | `serialHandlers.ts:522-541`，每包直接 `webContents.send` | HIGH |
107	| receive 全局处理锁 + 无界队列 | `receiveFramesStore.ts:806-904`，单锁序列化所有连接 | HIGH |
108	| 无 renderer-to-main backpressure | 无任何流控信号 | HIGH |
109	| `Array.from(data)` 序列化开销 | `networkHandlers.ts:603`，每包创建 number[] | MEDIUM |
110	| Promise double-resolve | `networkHandlers.ts:85-141`，timeout/error 可能双重 resolve | MEDIUM |
111	| `network:statusChange` 死通道 | preload 注册但 main 从未 emit | MEDIUM |
112	| UDP remote host 字符串解析脆弱 | 三处 split(':')，IPv6 不兼容 | MEDIUM |
113	| `autoReconnect` 配置无实现 | 类型定义存在但无代码 | LOW |
114	
115	这些风险只作为新桥接实现的反面证据，不作为目标架构。
116	
117	## 3. Real bridge implementation responsibilities
118	
119	### 3.1 Owner split
120	
121	| Owner | Real bridge implementation owns | Must not own |
122	| --- | --- | --- |
123	| `main` | serial port handle、TCP socket、TCP server、UDP socket 等 native resource handle；资源生命周期；OS I/O；per-connection input byte batching；write queue per live resource；subscription cleanup；bounded lifecycle/error event buffer；backpressure signal 当 queue 超限时；app shutdown resource drain | frame matching、receive classification、send task state、SCOE completion、result attribution、report generation、northbound error conversion、business device identity、TCP line splitting protocol semantics |
124	| `preload` | 最小 typed bridge，把 platform 允许的 transport capability 接到 main；IPC invoke 包装和 event subscription 管理；renderer 隔离边界 | 裸 `invoke/send/on`、任意 channel 订阅、`window.electron` 大包、业务 API |
125	| `platform` facade | renderer 侧 transport primitive 入口；discovery（枚举串口）、lifecycle command（connect/disconnect/cleanup）、byte I/O（write request）、event drain（拉取 main 缓冲事件）、backpressure query | 连接配置语义、target identity 语义、receive parsing、send result、task lifecycle |
126	| `connection` real adapter | 遵守 `ConnectionTransportAdapter` port；调用 platform facade transport primitive；把 platform technical material 映射为已有 connection service 输入；不暴露 platform resource handle | platform resource handle 直接暴露、API schema 定义权、业务帧解析 |
127	| `connection` service/state/core | 不变——real adapter 通过同一 port 接口接入，service/state/core 无需修改 | 任何因 real adapter 而增加的平台依赖 |
128	
129	### 3.2 Key split
130	
131	- main owns live resource facts needed to operate handles。
132	- connection owns canonical renderer-side connection facts。
133	- platform/preload own the bridge boundary, not business interpretation。
134	- runtime owns wiring and exception visibility。
135	
136	## 4. Port compatibility with fake adapter
137	
138	Real adapter 必须遵守 `ConnectionTransportAdapter` port 接口，不修改 port 定义。
139	
140	### 4.1 Port compatibility rules
141	
142	| Adapter method | Real adapter behavior | Fake adapter equivalent | Compatibility constraint |
143	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
144	| `connect(config)` | 通过 platform facade 调用 main IPC，main 创建 native resource | 内存 Map 记录 config | 接受相同的 `TransportConfig` 四种 kind；返回相同 `ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome` |
145	| `disconnect(id)` | 通过 platform facade 通知 main 关闭 native resource | 从 Map 删除 | 对已关闭资源返回 accepted with `disconnected` event；对不存在资源也返回 accepted（幂等） |
146	| `write(request)` | 通过 platform facade 发送 bytes 到 main | 记录到 writes Map | 返回 `write-accepted` 或 `write-failed`；携带 `byteLength` |
147	| `cleanup()` | 通过 platform facade 通知 main 关闭所有资源 | 清空 Map | 返回所有断开连接的 `disconnected` events |
148	| `drainEvents()` | 从 platform facade 拉取 main 缓冲的异步事件 | 从 eventQueue splice | 返回 `ConnectionAdapterEvent[]`；real adapter 的 main 缓冲等同 fake adapter 的 eventQueue |
149	
150	### 4.2 Gaps requiring port extension or platform supplement
151	
152	| Gap | Resolution strategy | Port change required |
153	| --- | --- | --- |
154	| Discovery（枚举串口） | platform facade 提供 `discoverResources(kind)` 方法，real adapter 在 connect 前调用；fake adapter 用 fixture 数据 | 不修改 port，discovery 在 adapter 外部通过 platform facade 完成 |
155	| Backpressure signal | main 在 queue 超限时 emit backpressure event category；real adapter 的 `drainEvents()` 返回该事件；fake adapter 通过 `pushError` 模拟 | 不修改 port，backpressure 作为新 `ConnectionAdapterEventKind` 或 `TransportErrorKind`；这是 schema 冻结前的候选类别 |
156	| TCP server multi-client | main 为每个 accepted client 生成独立 connection material；real adapter 把每个 client 作为独立 `connected` event | 不修改 port，但 connection service 需要处理多 client 场景 |
157	| UDP remote route change | main 在 remote route 可见性变化时 emit transport fact event | 不修改 port |
158	
159	### 4.3 Fake adapter extension plan
160	
161	Real adapter 到来前不需要改 fake adapter。但后续实现应补齐以下 fake adapter 场景：
162	
163	- backpressure 模拟：push backpressure event，让 service 测试 queue full 行为
164	- discovery 模拟：提供 `setResourceCandidates` 方法
165	- TCP server multi-client 模拟：`pushClientConnected`、`pushClientDisconnected`
166	- UDP remote route 变化模拟
167	
168	这些扩展不阻塞 real adapter 实现，但应在 real adapter 实现后逐步补齐。
169	
170	## 5. Event model
171	
172	### 5.1 Event classification
173	
174	Transport events 分为三类：lifecycle、data、backpressure/signal。
175	
176	| Category | Events | Frequency | Producer | Consumer | Design lane |
177	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
178	| Lifecycle | connected、disconnected、error（open-failed、connect-failed、listen-failed、bind-failed、timeout、close-failed、resource-unavailable、stale-event、invalid-config） | Low | main resource handler -> preload bridge -> platform facade -> connection adapter -> connection service | connection state、status read model、pages | Direct event delivery，no batching needed |
179	| Data | data（incoming bytes with connectionId、byteLength、source target） | High | main byte input handler -> batching in main -> preload bridge -> platform facade -> connection adapter drainEvents | connection service -> runtime route -> receive | Batched delivery，main-side buffer，separate from lifecycle |
180	| Backpressure/Signal | queue-full、queue-drained、write-backpressure（write queue full） | Low-Medium | main buffer management | connection adapter、runtime、status | Event-based signal，不自动转成业务错误 |
181	
182	### 5.2 Event flow
183	
184	```text
185	Main process:
186	  native resource event (serial data / socket data / socket close / socket error)
187	    -> main-side per-connection buffer:
188	       - lifecycle/error: immediate -> bounded lifecycle event queue
189	       - data: accumulate in byte batch buffer until window or flush trigger
190	    -> backpressure check:
191	       - if batch buffer exceeds threshold: emit queue-full signal
192	    -> typed event delivery to preload bridge
193	
194	Preload bridge:
195	  receives main events via IPC
196	  -> lifecycle events: immediate forward
197	  -> data events: forward batch as single event
198	  -> backpressure signals: forward as event
199	
200	Platform facade (renderer):
201	  wraps preload bridge API
202	  -> exposes drainEvents() for connection adapter
203	  -> exposes lifecycle command methods (connect/disconnect/write/cleanup)
204	
205	Connection real adapter:
206	  implements ConnectionTransportAdapter port
207	  -> drainEvents() pulls from platform facade
208	  -> maps platform events to ConnectionAdapterEvent[]
209	  -> no platform handle leakage
210	
211	Connection service:
212	  consumes adapter events via existing service logic
213	  -> routes data events to runtime for receive
214	  -> updates connection state for lifecycle events
215	  -> updates transport counters for all events
216	```
217	
218	### 5.3 Event ordering rules
219	
220	- 同一 connectionId 的 lifecycle 和 data 事件必须保序。
221	- 不同 connectionId 的事件可以交错，不需要全局序列号。
222	- `connected` event 必须在 data event 之前到达 service。
223	- `disconnected` event 后不应有同 connectionId 的 data event；若出现，connection service 标记为 `stale-event`。
224	- `write-accepted` 和 `write-failed` 是 command response 的一部分，不是异步 event queue 内容。
225	- Backpressure signal 不中断事件顺序，只是状态标记。
226	
227	### 5.4 Subscription lifecycle
228	
229	```text
230	Runtime startup:
231	  -> create connection service with real adapter
232	  -> adapter subscribes to platform facade event channel
233	  -> platform facade registers preload event listener
234	
235	Connection connect:
236	  -> service calls adapter.connect(config)
237	  -> adapter calls platform facade connect command (IPC invoke to main)
238	  -> main creates native resource, starts buffering
239	  -> main emits 'connected' event via preload bridge
240	  -> adapter drainEvents() picks up 'connected'
241	
242	App shutdown / page close:
243	  -> runtime calls service.cleanup()
244	  -> adapter calls platform facade cleanup command
245	  -> main drains all resources, closes handles
246	  -> platform facade removes event listener
247	  -> stale events after cleanup are discarded by adapter
248	```
249	
250	## 6. Queue / backpressure / batching principles
251	
252	### 6.1 Principles
253	
254	本节只定原则和验证点，不写最终参数。
255	
256	| Principle | Rationale | Verification point |
257	| --- | --- |--- |
258	| Main-side per-connection byte batch buffer | 减少 IPC 消息数量，避免旧系统的逐包穿透 | 验证 serial 115200 baud 和 TCP 高频场景下 IPC 消息速率下降 |
259	| Bounded batch buffer with flush trigger | 防止 batch buffer 无界增长 | 验证 buffer 达到上限时自动 flush，不等待时间窗口 |
260	| Time-window flush for low-frequency data | 低频数据不应因 batch 而延迟过长 | 验证空载或低频场景下 data event 在合理时间内到达 renderer |
261	| Bounded async event queue with overflow signal | eventQueue 不可无界 | 验证 queue 超限时 backpressure signal 到达 connection adapter |
262	| Overflow must be observable transport material | 丢弃不是静默行为 | 验证 overflow event 包含丢弃的 connectionId 和 byte count |
263	| Per-connection queue isolation | 一个连接的背压不应阻塞其他连接 | 验证 serial 和 TCP 同时高频时，serial 背压不影响 TCP 事件 |
264	| Main does not classify bytes | main 只做 buffer/batch/queue，不判断 frame content | 验证 main handler 代码不包含 frame matching、SCOE rule 或 receive classification |
265	| Overflow must not auto-convert to business error | 丢弃是 transport material，receive/send/task 自己决定业务含义 | 验证 overflow event 不进入 receive 作为 parse failure |
266	
267	### 6.2 Batch buffer design lane
268	
269	```text
270	Main per-connection byte input:
271	  native data callback
272	    -> append to per-connection byte buffer
273	    -> if buffer size >= maxBatchBytes: flush immediately
274	    -> else if time since last flush >= maxBatchWindowMs: flush
275	    -> flush: emit single 'data' event with accumulated bytes + source info
276	    -> if event queue depth >= maxQueueDepth: emit queue-full signal, apply drop policy
277	    -> drop policy candidate: drop oldest batch (latest data is more relevant for real-time systems)
278	```
279	
280	具体参数（maxBatchBytes、maxBatchWindowMs、maxQueueDepth）在 implementation 阶段根据 runtime evidence 和 hardware testing 确定。
281	
282	### 6.3 Write queue design lane
283	
284	```text
285	Main per-connection write:
286	  write request from renderer
287	    -> if native resource is live: write bytes directly
288	    -> if write callback indicates backpressure (drain event): queue subsequent writes
289	    -> if write queue exceeds threshold: return write-backpressure to adapter
290	    -> on drain: flush queued writes
291	```
292	
293	Write queue 与 input queue 独立管理，互不影响。
294	
295	### 6.4 What must NOT happen
296	
297	- 不可让 main 根据 byte content 决定 data 是否进入 renderer（旧 high-speed storage short-circuit 模式）。
298	- 不可让 batch window 导致低频数据无限延迟。
299	- 不可让 eventQueue 无界增长（旧 receiveFramesStore 模式）。
300	- 不可让一个连接的背压阻塞其他连接的事件传递（旧全局 processingLock 模式）。
301	- 不可让 renderer-to-main 的 IPC round-trip 成为每包处理的瓶颈（旧 receiveHandler invoke 模式）。
302	
303	## 7. Error and lifecycle mapping
304	
305	### 7.1 Lifecycle state mapping
306	
307	| Native event | Main emits | Adapter maps to | Connection lifecycle status |
308	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
309	| Serial port open success | connected event with target snapshot | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'connected' }` | `connected` |
310	| TCP socket connect success | connected event with target snapshot | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'connected' }` | `connected` |
311	| TCP server listen success | connected event with listener target snapshot | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'connected' }` | `connected` |
312	| UDP bind success | connected event with local bind target | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'connected' }` | `connected` |
313	| TCP server client accepted | connected event with client target snapshot | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'connected' }` + new target | `connected` for server, new target available |
314	| Native resource close | disconnected event | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'disconnected' }` | `disconnected` |
315	| Native resource error | error event with error kind | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'error' }` | `error` |
316	| User disconnect request | disconnected event after resource close | `ConnectionAdapterEvent { kind: 'disconnected' }` | `disconnected` |
317	| App shutdown | cleanup drains all resources | multiple `disconnected` events | all `disconnected` |
318	| Stale event after cleanup | discarded by adapter | not mapped | no change |
319	
320	### 7.2 Error kind mapping
321	
322	| Native error | Main emits error kind | Adapter maps to `TransportErrorKind` |
323	| --- | --- | --- |
324	| Serial port not found | `resource-unavailable` | `resource-unavailable` |
325	| Serial open rejected by OS | `open-failed` | `open-failed` |
326	| TCP connect refused/timeout | `connect-failed` or `timeout` | `connect-failed` or `timeout` |
327	| TCP server bind failed | `listen-failed` | `listen-failed` |
328	| UDP bind failed | `bind-failed` | `bind-failed` |
329	| Write to closed socket | `write-failed` + `resource-unavailable` | `write-failed` |
330	| Socket unexpected close | `disconnected` + optional error | `disconnected` |
331	| Serial device unplug | `disconnected` + error | `disconnected` + error |
332	
333	### 7.3 Error boundary rules
334	
335	- Transport error 只作为 connection fact 或 consumer input material。
336	- Write failure 只向 send/SCOE 提供 transport-level material，send/task/SCOE 自己决定业务含义。
337	- Disconnect 或 timeout 是 connection fact，不直接定义 result/report/northbound failure。
338	- Error visibility 属于 connection pages/status read model；UI 显示 sanitized summary，不泄漏 platform resource internals。
339	- 不让 main 根据 error kind 决定 task lifecycle、SCOE completion 或 report semantics。
340	
341	## 8. Main / preload / platform / connection forbidden surfaces
342	
343	### 8.1 Main forbidden surfaces
344	
345	- 禁止解析 frame、匹配 receive、构造 send result、推进 task、解释 SCOE、生成 report 或转换 northbound error。
346	- 禁止用 high-speed storage rule、receive rule 或 SCOE rule 决定 bytes 是否进入 renderer。
347	- 禁止全窗口无差别广播 raw high-frequency bytes。
348	- 禁止在 main handler 中做 TCP `\r\n` 分包——这是协议语义，归 receive/connection design。
349	- 禁止在 main 中维护业务统计（frame match count、task progress、SCOE completion count）。
350	- 禁止使用 `webContents.getAllWebContents()` 无差别广播。
351	
352	### 8.2 Preload forbidden surfaces
353	
354	- 禁止暴露裸 `invoke/send/on` 或任意 channel 订阅。
355	- 禁止暴露 `window.electron` 大包。
356	- 禁止暴露 receive processing、high-speed storage rule、task command、SCOE command 或 report API。
357	- 禁止在 preload 中做任何数据转换或协议判断。
358	
359	### 8.3 Platform facade forbidden surfaces
360	
361	- 禁止复制旧 `src/api/common/serialApi.ts` 或 `networkApi.ts` 的一一映射。
362	- 禁止暴露 raw socket、serialport handle 或 IPC channel 名。
363	- 禁止定义连接配置 schema、target id 格式或业务错误码。
364	- 禁止持有 mutable connection state。
365	
366	### 8.4 Connection adapter forbidden surfaces
367	
368	- 禁止暴露 platform resource handle、raw IPC channel 或 main process object。
369	- 禁止 import Node.js、Electron 或 serialport。
370	- 禁止解析 frame content、匹配 receive 或决定 task lifecycle。
371	- 禁止绑定到最终 preload/main schema 的具体 channel 名或 method 名——adapter 只通过 platform facade 抽象层交互。
372	
373	### 8.5 Connection service/state/core forbidden surfaces
374	
375	- 不因 real adapter 而增加任何平台依赖。
376	- core 保持纯 TypeScript，不 import Vue、Pinia、Electron、platform、Node、socket 或 serialport。
377	
378	## 9. Native dependency and packaged validation risk
379	
380	### 9.1 Native dependency risks
381	
382	| Dependency | Risk | Evidence | Mitigation |
383	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
384	| `serialport` + `@serialport/bindings-cpp` | C++ native addon 需要针对 Electron 35 重新编译 | 旧项目 `serialport@10.5.0`，Electron 35 使用较新 V8 | 升级到 serialport 12.x 或验证 rebuild 成功 |
385	| `electron-rebuild` | 旧项目有 `electron-rebuild@^3.2.9`，rewrite 无 | `rewrite/package.json` 无 rebuild script | 添加 `@electron/rebuild` devDep 和 rebuild script |
386	| Vite bundler externalization | 旧项目在 `rollupOptions.external` 中排除 serialport，rewrite 无 | `rewrite/quasar.config.ts` 无 external 配置 | 添加 serialport 相关 package 到 Vite external |
387	| `asarUnpack` | `asar: true` 但无 `asarUnpack` 配置 | `rewrite/quasar.config.ts:59` | 添加 `asarUnpack: ['**/*.node', '**/@serialport/**']` |
388	| Port enumeration | 旧项目用 Windows-only PowerShell registry query | `serialHandlers.ts:51-123` | 使用 `SerialPort.list()` API 实现跨平台枚举 |
389	| Build target | 旧项目支持 Windows (nsis)，rewrite 只有 Linux | `rewrite/quasar.config.ts` builder config | 若需 Windows 支持，添加 win target |
390	
391	### 9.2 Validation checkpoint matrix
392	
393	| Checkpoint | Package validation required | Hardware validation required | When to validate |
394	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
395	| serialport native module rebuild | YES | NO | 添加 serialport dep 后首次 build |
396	| Vite externalization | YES | NO | 修改 quasar.config.ts 后 |
397	| asar unpack configuration | YES | NO | 配置 asarUnpack 后 |
398	| Packaged app serial port loading | YES | YES | 首次 packaged build 后 |
399	| Real serial port enumerate | NO | YES | 开发态即可开始 |
400	| Real serial port open/close/read/write | NO | YES | 开发态即可开始 |
401	| Real TCP client connect/send/receive | NO | YES | 开发态即可开始 |
402	| Real TCP server listen/client accept/multi-client | NO | YES | 开发态即可开始 |
403	| Real UDP bind/send/receive/remote route | NO | YES | 开发态即可开始 |
404	| High-frequency batch/backpressure under real load | NO | YES | batch 参数确定后 |
405	| Cross-platform (Windows + Linux) serial behavior | NO | YES | Windows target 添加后 |
406	
407	### 9.3 Validation sequencing
408	
409	```text
410	Phase 1: Native dependency setup (package validation)
411	  -> add serialport dep
412	  -> add electron-rebuild script
413	  -> add Vite external
414	  -> verify dev-mode build succeeds
415	  -> verify dev-mode serial enumerate works
416	
417	Phase 2: Basic lifecycle (hardware validation, dev mode)
418	  -> real serial open/close
419	  -> real TCP client connect/close
420	  -> real TCP server listen/client accept/close
421	  -> real UDP bind/close
422	
423	Phase 3: Byte I/O (hardware validation, dev mode)
424	  -> real serial write/receive
425	  -> real TCP send/receive
426	  -> real UDP send/receive
427	
428	Phase 4: High-frequency and backpressure (hardware validation)
429	  -> batch buffer behavior under load
430	  -> queue overflow and backpressure signal
431	  -> multi-connection isolation
432	
433	Phase 5: Packaged app (package validation)
434	  -> packaged build with serialport
435	  -> verify asarUnpack works
436	  -> verify packaged app serial enumerate
437	  -> verify packaged app full lifecycle
438	
439	Phase 6: Multi-platform (if Windows target needed)
440	  -> Windows serial enumerate
441	  -> Windows packaged app behavior
442	```
443	
444	## 10. Runtime / package / hardware validation plan
445	
446	### 10.1 Runtime validation
447	
448	| Item | What to verify | Evidence needed |
449	| --- | --- | --- |
450	| App startup/shutdown wiring | connection service 和 adapter 正确创建和释放 | dev app 启动后连接状态正确，关闭后无泄漏 |
451	| Platform subscription cleanup | preload event listener 在 cleanup 后移除 | cleanup 后不再收到 stale event |
452	| Event ordering | connected before data, no data after disconnected | serial 和 TCP 连接后收发验证 |
453	| Fake/real adapter wiring | runtime 可在 fake 和 real adapter 间切换 | test env 用 fake, production 用 real |
454	| Batch buffer flush | 低频数据不因 batch 而无限延迟 | 单次发送后 data event 在合理时间内到达 |
455	| Multi-connection isolation | 多连接互不阻塞 | serial + TCP 同时高频收发 |
456	
457	### 10.2 Hardware validation
458	
459	| Transport | What to verify | Minimum evidence |
460	| --- | --- | --- |
461	| Serial | enumerate、open、close、reopen、write、receive、error、unplug | 至少一种真实串口设备的完整 lifecycle 记录 |
462	| TCP client | connect、send、receive、close、error、timeout | 对真实 TCP server 的完整 lifecycle 记录 |
463	| TCP server | listen、client accept、multiple client、per-client data、broadcast、client disconnect、server close cleanup | 至少 2 个 client 同时连接的记录 |
464	| UDP | bind、message、send、remote host route、broadcast candidate | 对真实 UDP target 的完整 lifecycle 记录 |
465	| High-frequency | batch behavior、queue overflow、backpressure signal、UI snapshot stability | 至少 baud rate 115200 serial 和 TCP 高频场景的吞吐和 UI 记录 |
466	
467	### 10.3 Package validation
468	
469	| Item | What to verify | Evidence needed |
470	| --- | --- | --- |
471	| Native module loading | packaged app 中 serialport 能正确 load | packaged app 启动不报 native module 错误 |
472	| asarUnpack | .node 文件在 asar 外可访问 | 检查 packaged app 解压后 asar.unpack 目录 |
473	| Resource path | packaged app 中 path resolve 正确 | packaged app 中 data path 可写 |
474	| Build target | Linux AppImage 和 deb 正确包含 native module | 安装 packaged app 后 serial enumerate 正常 |
475	
476	## 11. Implementation readiness gate
477	
478	### 11.1 Gate criteria
479	
480	进入 real bridge implementation 前必须满足：
481	
482	| # | Criterion | Status | Evidence |
483	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
484	| G1 | 实现轮重新列出 Direct contract / Boundary guards，并把本文列入 direct contract | 待满足 | 实现轮首行声明 |
485	| G2 | 明确本轮仅接受哪些 capability categories | 待满足 | 实现轮列出 discovery、lifecycle command、byte I/O、event drain、backpressure signal |
486	| G3 | 明确 owner split | 已满足 | 本文 §3 |
487	| G4 | 明确 real adapter 如何遵守 `ConnectionTransportAdapter` port | 已满足 | 本文 §4 |
488	| G5 | 明确 lifecycle/error/event 的 source、normalization owner、consumer 和 cleanup 责任 | 已满足 | 本文 §5、§7 |
489	| G6 | 明确 high-frequency queue/batch/backpressure 的设计状态 | 已满足（principle only） | 本文 §6，参数待 implementation/runtime evidence |
490	| G7 | 如 main 保留 buffer/backpressure，必须登记 runtime boundary exception | 已满足 | 本文 §6 即为 exception registry entry |
491	| G8 | 明确 validation levels | 已满足 | 本文 §9、§10 |
492	| G9 | 静态扫描确认 renderer 不直接访问 Node/Electron/raw IPC | 待满足 | 实现后 static scan |
493	| G10 | 未完成 runtime/package/hardware evidence 前，不声明真实连接链路验收完成 | 已满足 | 本文 §10 明确各层级 |
494	
495	### 11.2 Blockers
496	
497	进入 implementation 后仍存在以下 blocker，不阻塞开始实现但阻塞宣称完成：
498	
499	| Blocker | Blocks what | Unblock when |
500	| --- | --- | --- |
501	| 无 native dependency setup | packaged app serialport 行为 | 添加 serialport + electron-rebuild + Vite external + asarUnpack |
502	| 无 queue/batch/backpressure 参数 | high-frequency steady-state 声明 | runtime evidence 后确定参数 |
503	| 无 hardware test environment 记录 | hardware validation 声明 | 至少一种真实设备测试记录 |
504	| 无 Windows build target | Windows serial behavior 声明 | 添加 win target + 测试 |
505	| 无 TCP server multi-client hardware evidence | multi-client 声明 | 至少 2 client 硬件测试 |
506	| 无 high-speed storage short-circuit design | high-speed storage 与 transport 交互声明 | storage/receive/runtime 共同登记 exception |
507	
508	### 11.3 Cannot enter implementation when
509	
510	- 需要 final API schema（channel 名、method 名、DTO 字段）才能判断边界。
511	- 需要 receive/send/task/SCOE/result/report/northbound 语义才能写 transport bridge。
512	- 需要 customer `deviceId` 或 northbound schema 才能解释 serial/network target。
513	- 缺少 queue/backpressure 策略却要声明 high-frequency steady-state accepted。
514	- 缺少 package/native module 验证计划却要触达 packaged bridge loading。
515	
516	## 12. Deferred / blockers
517	
518	### 12.1 Deferred
519	
520	- Concrete platform/preload/main schema、channel、method、DTO、payload、error code、enum。
521	- Concrete connection config model（serial 额外选项、TCP keepAlive/noDelay、UDP broadcast）。
522	- Queue depth、batch window、overflow/drop policy、sampling、UI refresh interval、target throughput。
523	- TCP `\r\n` split ownership：transport input normalization、receive compatibility rule 或 candidate drop。
524	- High-speed storage short-circuit boundary with storage/receive/runtime。
525	- Global `webContents` broadcast replacement 的 target window/subscriber scoping 具体实现。
526	- Network status-change drift resolution。
527	- Serial option update while connected 的 UX 策略。
528	- UDP remote route representation（IPv6、duplicate route）。
529	- SCOE fixed TCP server / UDP source/target exception。
530	- Northbound `deviceId` and transport target separation。
531	- `autoReconnect` 策略（retry policy、cancellation、UI visibility）。
532	- Fake adapter backpressure/discovery/multi-client 扩展。
533	
534	### 12.2 Blockers for implementation
535	
536	- 无 accepted implementation design：本文即为此 gate，接受后解除。
537	- 无 native dependency setup：添加 serialport + rebuild + external + asarUnpack 后解除。
538	
539	### 12.3 Blockers for acceptance
540	
541	- Missing real serial/TCP/UDP validation blocks claiming hardware connection accepted。
542	- Missing runtime high-frequency evidence blocks claiming batching/backpressure accepted。
543	- Missing package validation blocks claiming packaged transport bridge/native module behavior accepted。
544	- Missing SCOE device evidence blocks claiming SCOE fixed source/target accepted。
545	- Missing customer schema/environment blocks claiming northbound delivery accepted。
546	
547	## 13. Readiness verdict
548	
549	`ready-for-bridge-implementation`，subject to gate criteria in §11.1。
550	
551	Reason：
552	
553	- Boundary owner split 已明确（§3）。
554	- Event model 已分类（§5）。
555	- Queue/backpressure principles 已定（§6），参数待 runtime evidence。
556	- Port compatibility 已分析（§4），real adapter 可遵守现有 port。
557	- Error and lifecycle mapping 已定义（§7）。
558	- Forbidden surfaces 已列出（§8）。
559	- Native dependency risk 已识别（§9.1）。
560	- Validation plan 已分层（§10）。
561	- Implementation readiness gate 已定义（§11）。
562	- Current rewrite implementation 有 fake connection contracts 和 adapter port，但无 real platform bridge。
563	- Real bridge implementation 可基于本文开始，前提是满足 §11.1 的 gate criteria 和 §11.2 的 blocker 前置条件。
564

8	
9	# Rewrite connection feature design
10	
11	## 1. Direct contract
12	
13	本设计只依据以下正式工件判断范围和完成度：
14	
15	1. `AGENTS.md`
16	2. `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-execution-charter.md`
17	3. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
18	4. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md`
19	5. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-boundaries.md`
20	6. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md`
21	7. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-transport-boundary.md`
22	8. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-platform-api-surface-reduction.md`
23	9. `codestable/quality/rewrite-validation-fixture-oracle-baseline.md`
24	10. `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
25	11. `codestable/quality/rewrite-review-checklist.md`
26	
27	`codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md` 仍是目录、依赖方向和 feature 归口的 canonical 架构基线。`rewrite-connection-transport-boundary.md` 是本 feature 的 connection/transport 直接边界输入。
28	
29	## 2. Boundary guards
30	
31	- 本轮是 Lane B 单 feature design，只产出 design/checklist。
32	- 本轮不实现 serial、TCP client、TCP server 或 UDP。
33	- 本轮不写 preload、main、renderer API schema，不冻结 channel、method、DTO、字段、错误码或枚举。
34	- connection owns transport config、runtime connection facts、transport-level lifecycle、error 和 event。
35	- connection does not own frame parsing、receive matching、send task state、SCOE semantics、result、report、northbound。
36	- main 可以承载平台 transport 资源、生命周期和必要 buffer / batch / queue / backpressure，不解释业务语义。
37	- renderer 不直接访问 Node、Electron、`ipcRenderer`、`fs`、`path`、`net`、`serialport` 或旧 `window.electron`。
38	- receive / send / task / SCOE / status / pages 只能通过 connection public boundary、runtime orchestration 或显式事件消费连接能力，不 import connection internal state。
39	- serial/network transport target 不等于 northbound `deviceId`、业务设备身份、task、case 或 report delivery target。
40	- 本轮不声明真实串口、TCP、UDP、SCOE、hardware、package、HTTP/FTP、northbound 或 customer validation 完成。
41	- 旧 `src/`、`src-electron/`、`public/` 只作为 legacy evidence、oracle 候选或 migration input，不作为新代码落点。
42	- 不读取或引用前端自动生成 types 文件作为证据。
43	
44	## 3. Evidence summary
45	
46	### 3.1 Contract evidence
47	
48	- target structure 将 `features/connection` 定义为串口/TCP/UDP 连接模型、连接实例、连接状态和 target 路由 owner；明确不拥有字节解析、业务结果、报告和任务生命周期。
49	- system architecture 和 feature boundaries 要求 renderer 只能经 `rewrite/src/platform` facade 访问桌面能力，main 只保留平台资源、生命周期和必要高频处理。
50	- feature interaction matrix 明确 `connection -> receive/send/SCOE/status` 是高风险交互：connection owns transport/source/target facts，receive/send/SCOE/status 分别拥有解析、发送结果、SCOE 语义和状态 read model。
51	- connection/transport boundary 已锁定 connection owns transport static config、runtime connection facts、target availability、transport events/errors/lifecycle，且只按能力类别声明 public API necessity。
52	- platform surface reduction 要求 serial/network 只作为 transport primitive 进入 platform；connection status、target route、device identity、receive parsing、send result 和 northbound `deviceId` 不归 platform。
53	- validation baseline 要求每个 feature 登记 legacy observable behavior ledger，并区分 static scan、Vitest unit、fixture test、oracle comparison、fake adapter test、manual checklist、runtime validation、hardware validation、package validation 和 customer validation。
54	
55	### 3.2 Legacy evidence
56	
57	本轮只读检索到的旧系统事实只用于保留可观测行为和识别迁移风险，不能自动升级为新架构结构：
58	
59	- 串口配置、扫描、连接、断开、状态、错误、收发和测试工具：`src/stores/serialStore.ts`、`src/components/connect/SerialPortList.vue`、`src/components/connect/SerialOptionsForm.vue`、`src/components/connect/SerialTestTools.vue`、`src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts`、`src-electron/preload/api/serial.ts`、`src/api/common/serialApi.ts`
60	- TCP client / TCP server / UDP 配置、连接、断开、发送、状态、事件和测试工具：`src/stores/netWorkStore.ts`、`src/components/connect/NetworkConnectionList.vue`、`src/components/connect/NetworkConnectionEditDialog.vue`、`src/components/connect/NetworkTestTools.vue`、`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts`、`src-electron/preload/api/network.ts`、`src/api/common/networkApi.ts`
61	- 连接页入口和可见 UI：`src/router/routes.ts`、`src/components/layout/SidePanel.vue`、`src/pages/ConnectConfigPage.vue`、`src/components/connect/ConnectionList.vue`、`src/components/connect/ConnectionContentPanel.vue`
62	- target 聚合和 send target 消费：`src/stores/connectionTargetsStore.ts`、`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts`
63	- receive / task / SCOE 消费连接事件和数据的旧耦合：`src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts`、`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskExecutor.ts`、`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts`、`src/stores/scoeStore.ts`、`src/composables/scoe/useScoeCommandExecutor.ts`
64	- 旧 Electron 暴露面：`src-electron/preload/index.ts`、`src-electron/preload/api/index.ts`、`src-electron/main/ipc/index.ts`、`src-electron/utils/common/ipcUtils.ts`、`src/api/common/index.ts`
65	- 当前 rewrite scaffold 只有最小 bridge info：`rewrite/src/platform/index.ts`、`rewrite/src/shared/platform-bridge.ts`、`rewrite/src-electron/preload/index.ts`
66	
67	## 4. Design
68	
69	### 4.1 Goals and non-goals
70	
71	Goals:
72	
73	- 保留旧系统连接页入口和串口、TCP client、TCP server、UDP 的用户可见连接能力。
74	- 锁定 connection 对 transport config、runtime connection facts、target availability、transport-level event/error/lifecycle 的单点 owner。
75	- 锁定 connection 与 platform/main/preload/runtime 的职责边界，防止旧 `window.electron` 大包、旧 `src/api/common` wrapper 和旧 main business handler 被照搬。
76	- 锁定 receive、send、task、SCOE、status、pages 等消费者如何读取连接能力，以及明确禁止的跨 feature 内部访问。
77	- 为后续 implementation 建立 fixture、fake adapter、manual、runtime、hardware、package、customer validation 计划。
78	
79	Non-goals:
80	
81	- 不实现 serial、TCP client、TCP server、UDP 或任何 platform bridge。
82	- 不写具体配置字段 schema、target id 格式、事件 payload、IPC channel、DTO、错误码或枚举。
83	- 不设计 receive parser、send frame builder、send task lifecycle、SCOE command / completion、result/report/northbound 内部语义。
84	- 不设计高速存储最终模型或 high-frequency 参数。
85	- 不把旧 `connectionTargetsStore`、`serialStore`、`netWorkStore`、旧 preload/API/main handler 的组织方式迁入 `rewrite/`。
86	- 不声明真实 hardware、package、HTTP/FTP、northbound 或 customer closure 完成。
87	
88	### 4.2 Owner / not owner
89	
90	| 分类 | connection owner | connection not owner |
91	| --- | --- | --- |
92	| transport config | 串口、TCP client、TCP server、UDP 的 transport static config 意义、默认输入应用边界、配置校验类别和可见配置快照。settings 可提供默认值，storage 只负责持久化技术边界。 | settings 默认值合并规则、storage 文件格式、northbound device identity、SCOE 业务配置。 |
93	| runtime facts | 连接实例、listener/client availability、remote host route、连接状态、last activity、transport counters、resource unavailable 等 transport 事实。 | receive parse result、send business result、task progress、SCOE tool record、status health summary、result truth、report file state。 |
94	| lifecycle | 连接请求、断开、重复连接、资源清理、关闭/重开、应用退出 cleanup、可选 reconnect 行为的 transport 生命周期规则。 | 任务开始/暂停/恢复/停止、SCOE 命令状态、report generation lifecycle、northbound transaction lifecycle。 |
95	| transport event | connected、disconnected、data available、write accepted、write failed、resource closed、resource error 等事件能力类别。 | frame matched、field parsed、send task completed、SCOE command success、case result、file delivered。 |
96	| error | port unavailable、open/connect/listen/bind failure、close failure、timeout、write failure、stale event、listener cleanup failure 等 transport-level error 类别。 | 外部错误码、拒绝执行语义、TestReport 错误、任务失败归因、SCOE completion failure 语义。 |
97	| public boundary | 面向 receive/send/task/SCOE/status/pages/runtime 的只读 selector、public service、显式事件和测试替换边界。 | 允许消费者 import connection internal state、adapter、store、Vue ref、platform resource handle 或 socket/serialport 对象。 |
98	| platform integration | connection adapter 通过 `rewrite/src/platform` facade 使用 transport primitive；main/preload 只提供受控 transport 能力。 | renderer 直接访问 Node/Electron/serialport/socket；platform 定义 connection config 语义或业务 target。 |
99	
100	connection 的核心职责是提供 transport 事实和 transport 能力边界。它不是 receive/send/task/SCOE 的总控制器，也不是 northbound 设备身份层。
101	
102	### 4.3 Legacy observable behavior ledger
103

105	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
