124	| 旧 SCOE 固定创建 `scoe-udp` 和 `scoe-tcp-server`，并在 receive/send 中硬编码固定 source/target。 | SCOE owns semantics; connection exposes transport availability | deferred to SCOE design | `src/stores/scoeStore.ts`、`src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts`、`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts` | runtime validation, hardware validation | 必须登记 SCOE fixed source/target exception。 |
125	| 旧 preload 暴露 `window.electron` 大包，renderer `src/api/common` 围绕大包组织 serial/network/receive 等能力。 | platform boundary evidence; connection must not inherit | candidate drop for exposure shape | `src-electron/preload/index.ts`、`src-electron/preload/api/index.ts`、`src/api/common/index.ts` | static scan | rewrite 只允许 narrow typed bridge + platform facade。 |
126	| 旧 serial/network data 和 connection event 向 renderer 广播，network 还广播到所有 webContents。 | platform/main event delivery; runtime scopes consumers | candidate drop for global broadcast shape | `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts`、`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts` | runtime validation | 新实现必须明确订阅生命周期和目标窗口/消费者范围。 |
127	| 当前 rewrite scaffold 只有 bridge info，没有 connection/transport 能力。 | platform + connection future work | not touched | `rewrite/src/platform/index.ts`、`rewrite/src/shared/platform-bridge.ts`、`rewrite/src-electron/preload/index.ts` | static scan | 本轮不声明连接链路完成。 |
128	
129	### 4.4 State owner
130	
131	| 状态类别 | 写入 owner | 读取方 | reset / lifecycle / persistence | 本轮设计约束 |
132	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
133	| Transport static config | connection owns config meaning; settings may provide defaults; storage/platform only persist or load through explicit boundary. | pages, runtime, settings, send, SCOE may read allowed config snapshot. | 低频更新；是否持久化由后续实现设计决定；缺失/非法旧值不能直接污染核心模型。 | 不冻结字段 schema；配置合法性和 target route 语义归 connection。 |
134	| Runtime connection facts | connection | send, receive, task, SCOE, status, pages, runtime via public selector/event. | 由 connect/disconnect/platform event/app cleanup 驱动；通常 transient；UI unmount 不等于事实 reset。 | 单点写入；main 是 raw resource event source，不是 renderer truth owner。 |
135	| Transport event read model | connection normalizes platform events; platform/main produces raw resource events. | receive consumes byte batches; send/SCOE consume write result/availability; status/pages consume lifecycle summary. | 事件订阅随 runtime 装配和 service 生命周期清理；是否保留 event log 后续设计。 | 不暴露 mutable state、socket、serialport、raw Electron object 或 raw channel。 |
136	| Target availability read model | connection | send、task、SCOE、pages、status | 由 runtime facts 派生；连接关闭、client 断开、UDP remote route 变化后更新。 | 只表达 transport target，不表达 customer device、task、case 或 report destination。 |
137	| Transport counters | connection for byte/connection-level counters only | pages/status may read summary; receive/send/status/result may maintain their own business stats. | 随连接生命周期 reset 或归档；是否持久化另行声明。 | counters 不成为 global business statistics，不写回 frame/settings/task/result。 |
138	| UI snapshot | connection pages/composables/widgets | UI only | 表单草稿、筛选、选中连接、测试工具输入、展开状态默认不持久化。 | UI snapshot 不能反向定义 connection facts。 |
139	| Runtime exception record | runtime owns registry; business owner feature owns semantics | reviewers, implementation tasks, affected features | 触达 high-frequency、SCOE fixed source/target、high-speed storage、package path 等例外时登记。 | connection design 只列候选，不把例外变成 main 业务授权。 |
140	
141	### 4.5 Transport lifecycle, event and error posture
142	
143	Connection lifecycle uses state concepts such as idle, connecting, connected, disconnecting, disconnected and error, but this design does not freeze enum names.
144	
145	Lifecycle rules:
146	
147	- connect / disconnect / cleanup must be idempotent enough for duplicate user actions, stale platform events and app shutdown.
148	- duplicate open/connect, close on already closed resource and app quit cleanup are preserved as observable lifecycle categories, not as exact implementation shape.
149	- changing serial options while connected is deferred; if preserved, close/reopen must be explicit and user-visible enough to validate.
150	- automatic reconnect is not assumed. If later implemented, it must define retry policy, cancellation, UI visibility and runtime/hardware validation in the implementation design.
151	- connection lifecycle events are low-frequency read model updates; high-frequency bytes must use separate batch/delta flow.
152	
153	Error rules:
154	
155	- Transport errors are connection facts until a consumer explicitly interprets them.
156	- Connection may normalize open/connect/listen/bind/write/timeout/close/resource-unavailable categories, but this document does not freeze error code names.
157	- A write failure can be returned to send/SCOE as transport-level material; send/task/SCOE decide business result or lifecycle under their own designs.
158	- A transport error must not directly define result/report/northbound failure semantics.
159	- Error visibility belongs to connection pages/status read model; UI displays sanitized summary, not platform resource internals.
160	
161	High-frequency rules:
162	
163	- Main may buffer, batch, throttle, queue or apply backpressure only for platform-resource or performance reasons.
164	- Main may not classify bytes as frame match, task trigger, SCOE success, result truth, report material or northbound delivery.
165	- connection may normalize source/target and byte availability; receive consumes byte batches and owns parse/match.
166	- UI must consume throttled snapshots and selectors, not raw serial/network event streams.
167	- Queue depth, batch window, overflow/drop policy, target throughput and UI refresh strategy are deferred to implementation/runtime evidence.
168	
169	### 4.6 Public API necessity
170	
171	connection needs a public API because receive, send, task, SCOE, status, pages and runtime all depend on transport availability. This is a boundary rule, not a required file shape and not a schema definition.
172	
173	Allowed capability categories only:
174	
175	- Transport config category: create, inspect, update and validate transport static config through controlled feature entry.
176	- Discovery category: expose available serial resources or transport resources as read-only candidates where platform supports discovery.
177	- Lifecycle command category: request connect, disconnect, cleanup and optional recovery actions.
178	- Target availability category: provide immutable target route, target status and availability snapshot for send/task/SCOE/pages.
179	- Byte input category: publish incoming byte batches or normalized transport input events to receive through runtime or explicit event.
180	- Byte output category: accept transport-level write requests from send/SCOE/runtime and return transport acceptance/failure material.
181	- Lifecycle/error event category: publish transport-level state and error events for status/pages/runtime.
182	- Test adapter category: allow fake serial/TCP/TCP server/UDP adapter substitution without exposing platform/main objects.
183	
184	Explicitly not connection public API:
185	
186	- receive frame match/process/result API.
187	- send frame build/business result API.
188	- task start/pause/resume/stop lifecycle API.
189	- SCOE command/complete/tool-record API.
190	- result/report/northbound projection or delivery API.
191	- mutable store, platform adapter, socket, serialport, raw Electron object, raw channel or DTO schema.
192	
193	### 4.7 Runtime involvement
194	
195	connection requires runtime involvement for application composition and cross-feature routing, but runtime must not own transport semantics.
196	
197	runtime may:
198	
199	- create and release connection service, connection state, fake/real transport adapters and platform subscriptions.
200	- route platform facade events into connection service and ensure listener cleanup.
201	- route connection byte batches to receive without letting receive import connection internals.
202	- route send/SCOE transport write requests to connection public service when the flow spans feature boundaries.
203	- combine read-only connection snapshot with status/page view model.
204	- coordinate cleanup when app exits, page scope closes, profile/settings change, hardware disconnects or stale events arrive.
205	- register boundary exceptions for high-frequency buffer/backpressure, high-speed storage short-circuit, SCOE fixed source/target, package/native-module behavior or optional northbound HTTP/FTP transport.
206	
207	runtime must not:
208	
209	- parse bytes, match frames or process fields.
210	- build send frames or define business send result.
211	- own task lifecycle or retry policy.
212	- interpret SCOE protocol or completion condition.
213	- define result, report, northbound or customer semantics.
214	- become a global service locator or hidden event bus.
215	- directly access Electron, Node, `serialport`, socket handles, file system or raw IPC.
216	
217	### 4.8 Platform / preload / main involvement
218	
219	Platform facade:
220	
221	- Is the renderer-side desktop capability boundary used by connection adapters.
222	- May expose transport primitive capability categories only after connection/platform implementation design accepts them.
223	- Does not own transport config semantics, target identity, receive parsing, send result, task lifecycle, SCOE protocol, result/report/northbound meaning.
224	- Must not reproduce old `src/api/common` as a one-to-one facade.
225	
226	Preload:
227	
228	- Exposes minimal typed bridge capabilities, not `window.electron`, not bare `invoke/send/on`, not arbitrary channel subscription.
229	- May bridge bounded serial/network lifecycle, byte I/O and event delivery after accepted capability design.
230	- Must not expose receive processing, high-speed storage business rules, task/SCOE commands, report semantics or northbound transaction semantics as platform abilities.
231	
232	Main process:
233	
234	- Owns live platform resources: serial port handles, TCP sockets, TCP server handles, UDP sockets, resource cleanup and OS-level I/O.
235	- May retain platform-side buffer, batch, queue, throttle, stream/file write or backpressure only when tied to platform resources or performance pressure.
236	- Must not own frame parsing, receive classification, task state transition, SCOE command/completion, result attribution, report generation, northbound error conversion or business device identity.
237	- Any main-side exception must record why renderer/TypeScript core cannot own it, what main retains, where business semantics live, validation required and exit condition.
238	
239	### 4.9 Cross-feature communication
240	
241	| Consumer / collaborator | May consume from connection | Must not do |
242	| --- | --- | --- |
243	| pages / connection UI | connection config snapshot, lifecycle commands, status/error summary, target list, test workflow entry. | access platform primitive directly, store runtime truth in page snapshot, subscribe to raw transport stream. |
244	| settings | provide defaults or persisted preference input if connection design accepts it. | own connection state, open serial/TCP/UDP, validate runtime availability, map target to northbound device. |
245	| receive | incoming byte batches, source snapshot, lifecycle/error material relevant to input availability. | import connection internal state, parse in connection, decide target availability, write connection facts. |
246	| send | target availability, transport write capability, write failure/acceptance material. | own connection lifecycle, hardcode SCOE/northbound targets, define task/result/report semantics. |
247	| task | target availability snapshot and transport failure material indirectly through send result or runtime. | query raw transport internals to mutate lifecycle, treat connection failure as task truth without task-owned rule. |
248	| SCOE | declared fixed source/target availability and write capability through runtime exception. | make connection own SCOE command semantics, hide SCOE fixed route in generic send/receive internals. |
249	| status | read-only connection summary, lifecycle/error events and transport health material. | own transport truth, import connection internal state, read raw platform resources. |
250	| storage / high-speed storage | only through explicit runtime exception when high-frequency file path is designed. | let connection call storage business rules, let main classify storage semantics without owner/exit condition. |
251	| result / report | no direct connection API in this feature. Future consumers may read transport failure material only through result/report design. | derive result/report truth directly from connection facts. |
252	| northbound | no direct transport target consumption. If external projection later needs connection state, it must go through status/task/result/report/northbound design. | treat serial/network target, TCP client, TCP server client or UDP remote host as customer deviceId. |
253	
254	Allowed interaction patterns:
255	
256	- public service for lifecycle commands, target query and transport write.
257	- read-only selector for config/status/target snapshots.
258	- explicit event for transport lifecycle/error and byte batches when async decoupling or batching is needed.
259	- runtime orchestration for connection + receive, connection + send/SCOE and lifecycle cleanup.
260	
261	Prohibited interaction patterns:
262	
263	- feature A imports connection internal state, adapter, composable, private service or platform bridge.
264	- connection imports receive/send/task/SCOE/status/result/report/northbound internal state to push business side effects.
265	- pages/widgets subscribe to raw platform transport events.
266	- status or task backfills connection truth by reading a shared global store.
267	
268	### 4.10 Target internal layering
269	
270	后续实现的目标分层只描述职责，不在本设计中冻结字段 schema。
271	
272	| 层 | 目标职责 |
273	| --- | --- |
274	| `core` | 纯 TypeScript 规则。负责 transport config validation category、connection lifecycle reducer、target availability derivation、event normalization、error normalization、selector projection 和 fake-event fixture。不得依赖 Vue、Pinia、Electron、platform、Node、socket、serialport 或全局 store。 |
275	| `services` | connection 用例入口。负责 connect/disconnect/cleanup/recovery candidate、target query、transport write request、event ingestion、状态更新和错误归一。通过显式 adapter port 与 platform 能力交互。 |
276	| `state` | connection runtime facts、transport read model、target availability、transport counters 和只读 selector。state action 保持薄层，不保存 platform adapter，不写其他 feature state。 |
277	| `adapters` | real/fake transport adapter。real adapter 调用 `rewrite/src/platform` facade；fake adapter 用于 fixture、Vitest 和 runtime wiring 测试。adapter 不解释 receive/send/task/SCOE 语义。 |
278	| `composables` | 面向 connection 页面和局部组件的 UI-facing 组合。负责表单草稿、测试工具输入、订阅生命周期、错误展示、selector 组合和 service 调用。 |
279	| `components` | 连接列表、串口配置、网络配置、状态卡片、测试工具等 feature UI。组件通过 props/events/composables 使用 connection 能力，不直接访问 platform 或其他 feature internals。 |
280	| `fixtures` | serial/TCP/TCP server/UDP config 样本、fake platform events、target availability expected output、error/lifecycle transition cases、高频小样本和 legacy observable behavior oracle。fixtures 是证据材料，不是新核心 schema。 |
281	
282	### 4.11 Structure health and micro-refactor stance
283	
284	本轮不做代码实现，也不做“只搬不改行为”的微重构。
285	
286	后续实现应在 `rewrite/src/features/connection` 建立必要层，不迁移旧 `src/stores/serialStore.ts`、`src/stores/netWorkStore.ts`、`src/stores/connectionTargetsStore.ts`、旧 `src/api/common/serialApi.ts`、`networkApi.ts` 或旧 main/preload handler 的组织方式。
287	
288	旧代码中的职责混杂只作为实现风险：
289	
290	- `connectionTargetsStore` 聚合 serial/network target 并被 send/SCOE/storage 等消费。
291	- `serialStore`、`netWorkStore` 直接把 data event 推入 receive store。
292	- `networkHandlers` 同时处理 transport、TCP line splitting、高速存储 short-circuit 和全窗口广播。
293	- 旧 preload/API 暴露面以 `window.electron` 和 `src/api/common` 为中心，不符合 rewrite platform facade。
294	
295	这些问题不阻塞本 design，但必须进入后续 implementation checklist、runtime exception registry 和 review。
296	
297	### 4.12 Validation plan
298	
299	Static scan:
300	
301	- 检查 design/checklist 章节、direct contract、boundary guards、ledger、owner/state/public API/runtime/platform/cross-feature/validation/deferred 是否存在。
302	- 后续实现时检查 renderer 无 Node/Electron/`window.electron`/raw IPC import，feature 之间不 import connection internal state，platform 不复刻 `src/api/common`。
303	
304	Vitest unit:
305	
306	- core lifecycle reducer、config validation category、target availability derivation、error/event normalization、selector projection。
307	- state read model update from fake platform events。
308	- no Electron/Node/platform dependency in core.
309	
310	Fixture test:
311	
312	- serial/TCP/TCP server/UDP config samples and invalid samples.
313	- duplicate connect、disconnect already closed、stale event、write failure、timeout、listener cleanup、close/reopen candidate.
314	- target availability for serial target、TCP target、TCP server listener/client and UDP remote route.
315	- TCP line split compatibility sample as deferred boundary material, not final receive schema.
316	
317	Oracle comparison:
318	
319	- Legacy observable behavior ledger can seed golden cases for visible config/defaults, target route projection, lifecycle transition and error visibility.
320	- Old UI screenshots/logs may be added later as manual oracle; not required in this pure design turn.
321	
322	Fake adapter test:
323	
324	- fake serial list/open/close/write/data/error/close ordering.
325	- fake TCP connect/data/error/close/timeout.
326	- fake TCP server listen/client connect/client close/broadcast write.
327	- fake UDP bind/message/send/error/close/remote route.
328	- failure injection for disconnect during write, duplicate close, stale event and cleanup.
329	
330	Manual checklist:
331	
332	- `/connect` and sidebar entry reachable.
333	- serial/network config create/edit/delete/select visible.
334	- connect/disconnect/reconnect/test actions expose coherent button states and errors.
335	- status, last activity and target availability visible without exposing internal state.
336	- send/SCOE target selection visible through allowed snapshot.
337	
338	Runtime validation:
339	
340	- app startup/shutdown wiring, platform subscription cleanup, event ordering, fake/real bridge lifecycle.
341	- high-frequency byte batches, throttled UI snapshots and no raw event storm.
342	- disconnect during high-frequency data and during write.
343	- runtime exception records when high-speed storage, SCOE fixed route or package/native-module behavior is touched.
344	
345	Hardware validation:
346	
347	- real serial enumerate/open/close/reopen/send/receive/error/unplug order.
348	- real TCP client connect/send/receive/close/error/timeout.
349	- real TCP server listen/client accept/multiple client send/close/server close cleanup.
350	- real UDP bind/send/receive/broadcast candidate and remote host routing.
351	- real throughput/backpressure behavior after parameters are designed.
352	
353	Package validation:
354	
355	- only required when implementation touches packaged native module behavior, platform bridge loading, resource path, extraResources or packaged runtime differences.
356	- package validation cannot be replaced by development Electron runtime or Vitest.
357	
358	Customer validation:
359	
360	- not applicable to connection acceptance except as a blocker guard: connection must not map transport target to customer `deviceId`.
361	- northbound HTTP/FTP, TestReport, customer error/refusal/status semantics remain outside connection.
362	
363	Cannot claim from this design:
364	
365	- real serial/TCP/UDP hardware chain complete.
366	- high-frequency steady-state complete.
367	- SCOE fixed source/target complete.
368	- packaged transport bridge complete.
369	- HTTP/FTP/northbound delivery complete.
370	- customer closure complete.
371	
372	### 4.13 Deferred / blockers
373	
374	Deferred:
375	
376	- Concrete transport config model, target id representation, persistence shape and migration rule.
377	- Concrete preload/main/renderer API schema, channel/method names, DTOs, event payloads, error codes and enum names.
378	- serial option update while connected: preserve close/reopen behavior, change UX, or require manual reconnect.
379	- TCP `\\r\\n` split ownership: transport input normalization, receive compatibility rule or candidate drop.
380	- queue depth、batch window、overflow/drop policy、backpressure、sampling、UI refresh interval and target throughput.
381	- network status-change drift: old preload exposes status-change but main evidence primarily uses connection event/data event.
382	- global webContents broadcast replacement and target window/subscriber scoping.
383	- high-speed storage short-circuit boundary with storage/receive/runtime.
384	- SCOE fixed TCP server / UDP source/target exception.
385	- UDP remote host route representation, including IPv6 and duplicate route behavior.
386	- whether old network `autoReconnect` option is real behavior, future strategy or dead config candidate.
387	- northbound `deviceId` and transport target separation in customer-facing flows.
388	
389	Blockers for implementation:
390	
391	- No accepted implementation design for concrete platform transport bridge capabilities.
392	- No runtime evidence for real serial/TCP/UDP event order and failure behavior.
393	- No high-frequency performance target or overflow/backpressure policy.
394	- No hardware validation environment recorded for SCOE fixed source/target.
395	- No customer materials for northbound device identity, result/report/file delivery closure.
396	
397	Blockers for acceptance:
398	
399	- Missing real serial/TCP/UDP validation blocks claiming hardware connection accepted.
400	- Missing runtime high-frequency evidence blocks claiming batching/backpressure accepted.
401	- Missing package validation blocks claiming packaged transport bridge/native module behavior accepted.
402	- Missing SCOE device evidence blocks claiming SCOE fixed source/target accepted.
403	- Missing customer schema/environment blocks claiming northbound delivery accepted.
404	
405	## 5. Checklist entry
406	
407	后续 `cs-feat-impl` 入口以 `codestable/features/rewrite-connection/rewrite-connection-checklist.yaml` 为准。实现阶段必须先重新确认本设计中的 direct contract、boundary guards、legacy observable behavior ledger、owner/not owner、state owner、public API necessity、runtime/platform involvement、cross-feature communication、validation plan 和 deferred/blocker 项，不能把本轮文档外的旧代码结构升级为新实现合同。
408

15	
16	# Rewrite connection / transport boundary
17	
18	## 1. Scope
19	
20	本轮是东方红上位机重写的 connection / transport foundation boundary design。
21	
22	Lane judgement:
23	
24	- Lane B。
25	- 本轮只做 foundation boundary design。
26	- 不写业务代码。
27	- 不实现 serial、TCP client、TCP server 或 UDP。
28	- 不写 preload / main / renderer API schema。
29	- 不进入 receive / send / task / SCOE / result / report / northbound 内部设计。
30	
31	Direct contract:
32	
33	- `AGENTS.md`
34	- `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-execution-charter.md`
35	- `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-scope-default-preserve.md`
36	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
37	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md`
38	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-boundaries.md`
39	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md`
40	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-shared-tooling-audit-plan.md`
41	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-pre-design-gate-and-sequencing.md`
42	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-platform-api-surface-reduction.md`
43	- `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
44	- `codestable/quality/rewrite-review-checklist.md`
45	- 当前 `rewrite` scaffold 相关文件：`rewrite/src-electron/main`、`rewrite/src-electron/preload`、`rewrite/src/platform`、`rewrite/src/shared`
46	
47	Boundary guards:
48	
49	- renderer 不直接访问 Node、Electron、`ipcRenderer`、`fs`、`path`、`net`、`serialport`。
50	- preload 只暴露 typed bridge，不暴露裸 `invoke/send/on`。
51	- main 可以拥有串口、网络 socket、连接生命周期、必要缓冲、队列和背压，但不解释业务帧、字段、任务、报告语义。
52	- `connection` 只拥有连接配置、连接运行事实、连接错误、连接生命周期和 transport-level event。
53	- `connection` 不拥有 frame parsing、receive matching、send task state、SCOE protocol semantics、result、report、northbound。
54	- receive / send / task / SCOE 只能通过 connection public service 或 runtime 编排消费连接能力，禁止 import connection 内部 state。
55	- 本文不冻结具体 channel、method、DTO、错误码、枚举。
56	- 本文不声明真实串口、TCP、UDP、SCOE 硬件链路完成。
57	
58	## 2. Legacy evidence summary
59	
60	旧系统事实只用于识别可观测行为、迁移风险和 oracle 候选，不能自动升级为目标架构。
61	
62	### 2.1 Serial evidence
63	
64	- 旧 main 串口 handler 负责端口枚举、打开、关闭、写入、读取、状态查询、选项设置和事件广播。`src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:130`, `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:286`, `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:513`, `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:580`
65	- 旧 main 串口打开时创建 `SerialPort` 并绑定 `ReadlineParser`，说明旧链路已在 transport 层带有按行 parser 倾向；新架构不能因此把业务解析放进 main。`src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:154`, `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:164`
66	- 旧 serial data 事件直接广播到所有窗口，并同步更新字节计数和状态。`src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:521`, `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:531`, `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts:567`
67	- 旧 renderer 的 `serialStore` 通过 `serialAPI.open` 执行连接，成功后注册 listener，并维护连接状态、活动端口、错误和历史消息。`src/stores/serialStore.ts:161`, `src/stores/serialStore.ts:177`, `src/stores/serialStore.ts:191`
68	- 旧 serial 入站数据在 store 中进入 `receiveFramesStore.handleReceivedData('serial', portPath, data)`。`src/stores/serialStore.ts:392`, `src/stores/serialStore.ts:414`
69	- 旧串口目标通过字符串 `serial:${port.path}` 进入发送目标路由。`src/stores/connectionTargetsStore.ts:56`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:105`
70	
71	Evidence judgement:
72	
73	- 旧系统可观测能力包括串口枚举、连接、断开、收发、状态展示、错误展示、页面测试发送和作为 send target。
74	- 旧字符串 target、逐包事件穿透、main 侧 parser 倾向和 store 直接调用 receive 是迁移风险，不是目标结构。
75	
76	### 2.2 TCP client / TCP server / UDP evidence
77	
78	- 旧 network 运行路径覆盖 TCP client、TCP server 和 UDP，renderer store 通过 `networkAPI.connect / disconnect / send` 调用 main。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:85`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:146`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:229`, `src/stores/netWorkStore.ts:50`
79	- TCP client 使用 `net.Socket`，连接后发送 connection event；`error`、`close`、`timeout` 更新状态或清理连接记录。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:85`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:100`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:120`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:128`
80	- TCP server 使用 `net.createServer` 接收 client socket，并维护 server client 列表。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:146`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:155`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:162`
81	- UDP 使用 `dgram.createSocket('udp4')`、本地 bind、`message` 事件和可选 broadcast。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:229`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:247`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:265`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:268`
82	- TCP 入站先按 `\r\n` 拆包再进入通用 data received；UDP message 直接进入通用 data received。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:464`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:487`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:498`
83	- 旧 network handler 在 data received 中先判断 high-speed storage rule，命中后异步存储并不再广播给 renderer。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:505`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:509`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:515`
84	- network data 和 connection event 直接广播给所有 renderer web contents。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:582`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:598`
85	- 旧 network 入站数据在 store 中进入 `receiveFramesStore.handleReceivedData('network', connectionId, data)`。`src/stores/netWorkStore.ts:207`, `src/stores/netWorkStore.ts:210`
86	
87	Evidence judgement:
88	
89	- 旧系统可观测能力包括 TCP client、TCP server、UDP 本地绑定、UDP remote host、连接事件、数据收发和页面测试发送。
90	- 旧 TCP 的 `\r\n` 分包、high-speed storage 短路、状态事件漂移和发送无明确背压策略都是后续 feature design 的风险证据。
91	- TCP client、TCP server、UDP 的 transport 可保留为 capability，但旧 network handler 的业务分流不能进入目标 main 职责。
92	
93	### 2.3 Receive / send / task / SCOE consumption evidence
94	
95	- 旧 receive 总入口支持 `serial | network` source，并使用处理锁和队列保护。`src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:882`, `src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:887`
96	- 旧 receive 对 `scoe-tcp-server` 有固定 source 特判。`src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1021`
97	- 旧 receive 成功后会继续处理接收 API 结果、星座图数据和 trigger task 条件。`src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1198`, `src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1215`, `src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1252`
98	- 旧 receive trigger 会直接调用 `sendTasksStore.handleFrameReceived`。`src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1263`, `src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1293`
99	- 旧 send 统一发送器把 targetId 解析为 `serial` 或 `network`，再分别走串口或网络发送。`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:31`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:100`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:105`
100	- 旧 UDP remote host 目标通过 `connectionId:host:port` 字符串拼接继续向 network API 传递。`src/stores/connectionTargetsStore.ts:190`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:118`
101	- 旧 send 成功后直接更新 send stats、global stats，并硬编码 SCOE UDP target 记录。`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:146`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:150`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:154`
102	- 旧 task 发送失败后再查询 target availability，不可用时把任务置为 paused。`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskExecutor.ts:229`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskExecutor.ts:233`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskExecutor.ts:236`
103	- 旧 SCOE 直接创建固定网络连接 `scoe-udp` 和 `scoe-tcp-server`，发送固定走 `network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote`。`src/stores/scoeStore.ts:216`, `src/stores/scoeStore.ts:252`, `src/stores/scoeStore.ts:278`
104	
105	Evidence judgement:
106	
107	- receive、send、task、SCOE 都需要消费连接能力，但旧代码把连接状态、target 路由、receive 解析、task trigger、SCOE 固定目标和统计副作用揉在多处 store/composable 中。
108	- 新架构必须把 connection public service、runtime orchestration 和 explicit event 作为边界，禁止 feature import connection 内部 state。
109	
110	### 2.4 Electron exposure evidence
111	
112	- 旧 preload 暴露统一 `window.electron` 大包。`src-electron/preload/index.ts:1`, `src-electron/preload/index.ts:7`
113	- 旧 preload 聚合 window、menu、autoLaunch、serial、network、files、dataStorage、path、receive、highSpeedStorage、historyData、timerManager。`src-electron/preload/api/index.ts:1`, `src-electron/preload/api/index.ts:14`
114	- 旧 renderer `src/api/common` 直接围绕 `window.electron` 包装 serial / network / files / path / receive 等能力。`src/api/common/serialApi.ts:12`, `src/api/common/networkApi.ts:18`
115	- 当前 `rewrite` scaffold 仍是最小 typed bridge：preload 只暴露 bridge info，platform facade 只读取 bridge info，capabilities 为空。`rewrite/src-electron/preload/index.ts:1`, `rewrite/src/platform/index.ts:1`, `rewrite/src/shared/platform-bridge.ts:1`
116	
117	Evidence judgement:
118	
119	- 旧三段式 main / preload / renderer 可以作为能力清单 evidence。
120	- 旧 `window.electron` 大包、旧 `src/api/common` wrapper、旧 main business handler 不能作为新 API 形状。
121	- 当前 `rewrite` scaffold 尚未实现 connection feature 或 transport API；本轮不能声明连接链路完成。
122	
123	## 3. Owner / not owner
124	
125	### 3.1 Transport resource owner and runtime facts owner
126	
127	| Transport | Platform / main resource responsibility | Connection feature responsibility | Runtime facts owner | Not owner |
128	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
129	| Serial | 端口枚举、打开、关闭、底层读写、底层错误和关闭事件、必要平台侧 buffer / batch / backpressure | 串口连接配置模型、连接实例、状态事实、target availability、transport-level error、生命周期事件归一 | `features/connection` owns canonical connection facts; main only holds live resource facts | frame parsing、receive matching、send task state、SCOE semantics、report、northbound device identity |
130	| TCP client | socket 创建、连接、断开、底层读写、socket error / close / timeout、必要平台侧 buffer / batch / backpressure | TCP client 连接配置模型、连接实例、状态事实、target availability、transport-level error、生命周期事件归一 | `features/connection` owns canonical connection facts; main only holds socket resource facts | 上层协议拆帧、任务生命周期、业务结果、外部 device identity |
131	| TCP server | server listen / close、client socket accept / close、底层读写、client resource cleanup、必要平台侧 buffer / batch / backpressure | TCP server listener 与 client availability 的 transport facts、server target 可用性、生命周期事件归一 | `features/connection` owns listener/client transport facts visible to renderer; main owns live server/socket handles | 业务会话语义、SCOE 命令含义、receive 分类、northbound session |
132	| UDP | socket bind / close、message event、send、broadcast option、必要平台侧 buffer / batch / backpressure | UDP 本地绑定连接、remote host target route、target availability、transport-level error、生命周期事件归一 | `features/connection` owns canonical UDP connection and remote host route facts; main owns live socket resource facts | frame parsing、remote business device identity、SCOE command completion、northbound deviceId |
133	
134	Rules:
135	
136	- `connection` 是 transport domain owner；`platform/main` 是平台资源 owner。
137	- main 可以产生 raw platform facts，但 canonical renderer-side connection facts、target availability 和 transport read model 归 `connection`。
138	- `connection` 的 target identity 只表示 transport target，不表示 northbound `deviceId`、业务设备、case、task 或 SCOE 领域对象。
139	- TCP server 的 client 连接可以是 transport-level availability material，但不能自动成为业务会话或 northbound 设备。
140	- UDP remote host 是 transport route material，不冻结字段 schema，也不等同 customer device。
141	
142	### 3.2 Connection owns
143	
144	`connection` owns:
145	
146	- serial / TCP client / TCP server / UDP 的 connection profile 和 transport static config。
147	- 连接实例生命周期：idle、connecting、connected、disconnecting、disconnected、error 等状态概念的归口。本文不冻结枚举名称。
148	- target route 和 target availability 的 read model。
149	- transport-level event：connected、disconnected、error、data available、write accepted、write failed、resource unavailable 等能力类别。本文不冻结事件名称或 payload。
150	- transport-level error：端口不存在、打开失败、socket 关闭、timeout、写入失败、资源释放失败等归一类别。本文不冻结错误码。
151	- 平台事件到 connection read model 的归一规则。
152	- 面向 receive / send / task / SCOE / status / runtime 的公开能力类别。
153	- fake transport adapter 所需的测试替换边界。
154	
155	### 3.3 Connection does not own
156	
157	`connection` must not own:
158	
159	- frame byte parsing、identifier matching、field extraction、expression input 或 receive result。
160	- send frame construction、send instance stats、send task lifecycle 或 task progress。
161	- SCOE protocol semantics、command parse、completion condition、tool record 或 fixed source / target 的业务含义。
162	- result truth、report object、history/CSV/report material、northbound delivery、HTTP/FTP transaction。
163	- northbound `deviceId`、subsystem identity、customer-facing task/control/status/result/error semantics。
164	- UI page state、dialog state、form dirty state、table pagination 或 chart refresh state。
165	
166	## 4. State owner
167	
168	| State layer | Owner | Write rule | Readers | Persistence |
169	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
170	| Transport static config | `features/connection` owns transport config meaning. `settings` may own user defaults. `storage` only persists snapshots through explicit feature request. | Config changes go through connection public service or runtime setup. Platform/main does not define config semantics. | pages, settings, send, SCOE, runtime may read allowed config snapshot. | Persist only when feature design says so; storage owns file I/O, not config meaning. |
171	| Runtime connection facts | `features/connection` | Single writer based on explicit user command, runtime lifecycle command, or platform event. main is source of resource events, not renderer truth owner. | send, receive, task, SCOE, status, pages via public selectors or runtime. | Usually transient; feature design must mark whether any reconnect/session facts persist. |
172	| Transport event / read model | `features/connection` for normalized transport events and read model; platform/main for raw resource event production. | Raw events enter through platform facade, then connection normalizes. Data batches may be forwarded through runtime to receive without exposing internal state. | receive consumes byte batches; send/SCOE consume write result and target availability; status consumes read model. | Event log/history is not implicit; if persisted, storage owns persistence only. |
173	| UI snapshot | pages / feature composables / widgets | UI can filter, group, throttle, paginate, and display; UI cannot write runtime facts. | UI components only. | UI preference persistence belongs settings only when explicitly designed. |
174	
175	Rules:
176	
177	- Static config, runtime facts, transport read model, UI snapshot must stay separate.
178	- `connection` read model cannot expose mutable internal state references.
179	- Runtime facts are reset by connection lifecycle rules, not by UI unmount alone.
180	- send / receive / task / SCOE must not infer connection facts by importing connection store internals.
181	- Transport counters may exist as connection read model, but business statistics belong to the corresponding feature owner.
182	
183	## 5. Platform / preload / main / renderer boundary
184	
185	Renderer:
186	
187	- Uses only `rewrite/src/platform` facade for desktop capability access.
188	- Does not access `window.electron`, Electron, Node, `ipcRenderer`, `fs`, `path`, `net` or `serialport`.
189	- Does not subscribe directly to raw transport events from preload.
190	- Pages and widgets call connection public service, feature composables, or runtime page-level entry, not platform transport primitives.
191	
192	Platform facade:
193	
194	- Lives in renderer and hides preload details from features.
195	- Exposes capability categories only after corresponding foundation / feature design accepts the capability.
196	- Can expose transport primitives to connection adapters, not to arbitrary pages or features.
197	- Does not own connection config semantics, target identity, receive parsing, send result semantics, task lifecycle, SCOE protocol, result/report/northbound meaning.
198	
199	Preload:
200	
201	- Exposes a minimal typed bridge.
202	- Does not expose raw `invoke/send/on`, arbitrary channel subscription, or all-in-one `window.electron`.
203	- Does not expose `receive` business processing, high-speed storage business rules, history/report semantics, or task/SCOE commands as platform abilities.
204	- For transport, preload may bridge bounded lifecycle, byte I/O and bounded event delivery only after the connection design fixes the capability category.
205	
206	Main process:
207	
208	- Owns platform resources: serial port handles, TCP sockets, TCP server handles, UDP sockets, resource cleanup, app lifecycle, and necessary OS-level I/O.
209	- May retain platform-side buffering, batching, throttling, queueing, stream/file writing and backpressure when tied to platform resources or performance pressure.
210	- Must not define frame parsing, receive classification, task state transitions, SCOE completion, result attribution, report generation, northbound error conversion or business device identity.
211	- Any main-side exception must record why renderer / TypeScript core cannot reasonably own it, what main retains, where business semantics live, and how validation is done.
212	
213	Connection feature:
214	
215	- Is the renderer-side transport domain owner.
216	- Consumes platform transport primitives through adapters.
217	- Normalizes platform facts into transport facts.
218	- Publishes only public service, read-only selector, explicit event, or runtime-facing capabilities.
219	- Does not expose internal state to receive / send / task / SCOE.
220	
221	## 6. Connection public API necessity
222	
223	`connection` needs a public API because receive, send, task, SCOE, status, pages and runtime all depend on transport availability. This public API is a boundary rule, not a required file shape and not a schema definition.
224	
225	Allowed capability categories:
226	
227	- Lifecycle command category: request connect, disconnect, reconnect attempt, resource cleanup, and lifecycle recovery. This does not define method names.
228	- Config query / update category: read or update transport static config through controlled entry; settings may provide defaults, but connection owns transport meaning.
229	- Target selector category: read target availability, target route, connection kind, and status snapshot as immutable read model.
230	- Byte input category: expose incoming transport byte batches or normalized transport input events to receive through runtime or explicit event.
231	- Byte output category: accept transport-level write requests from send / SCOE through public service or runtime; return transport-level acceptance or failure, not business send result.
232	- Error / lifecycle event category: publish transport-level lifecycle and error events for status, pages and runtime.
233	- Test adapter category: allow fake transport adapter injection in tests without exposing platform/main objects.
234	
235	Rules:
236	
237	- Public API must not leak mutable connection state, platform resource handles, raw Electron objects, raw socket instances or serialport instances.
238	- Public API must not freeze DTO, channel names, error code names or enum values in this document.
239	- Public API must not make receive / send / task / SCOE depend on connection store implementation.
240	- If a future feature has no external connection consumer, no extra public API is required for that feature.
241	- If a future flow requires cross-feature write or lifecycle progression, it must state owner, caller, input/output boundary, failure handling and validation level in its feature design.
242	
243	## 7. Runtime involvement
244	
245	`runtime/` is needed only as an application composition and cross-feature orchestration layer.
246	
247	Runtime may:
248	
249	- Create and release connection service, transport adapters and platform subscriptions at application startup and shutdown.
250	- Wire platform facade events into connection service.
251	- Route connection byte batches to receive without letting receive import connection internals.
252	- Route send / SCOE transport write requests to connection public service when the flow spans feature boundaries.
253	- Coordinate lifecycle cleanup when pages close, app exits, user switches profiles, or hardware disconnects.
254	- Register boundary exceptions such as high-frequency buffer / batch / backpressure, high-speed storage short-circuit, SCOE fixed source/target, packaged data path or optional HTTP/FTP transport.
255	- Combine read-only connection snapshot with status or page view model.
256	
257	Runtime must not:
258	
259	- Parse receive bytes.
260	- Build send frames.
261	- Own task lifecycle.
262	- Interpret SCOE protocol.
263	- Define result, report or northbound semantics.
264	- Become a global service locator or hidden event bus.
265	- Directly access Electron, Node, `serialport`, socket handles or file system.
266	
267	## 8. High-frequency data principles
268	
269	Target high-frequency flow:
270	
271	```text
272	platform resource
273	  -> main-side buffer / batch / queue / backpressure when justified
274	  -> typed preload bridge
275	  -> platform facade
276	  -> connection adapter and connection read model
277	  -> runtime route or explicit event
278	  -> receive / send / storage / SCOE public boundary
279	  -> feature read model delta
280	  -> throttled UI snapshot
281	```
282	
283	Rules:
284	
285	- Main may batch, buffer, throttle, queue or apply backpressure for platform-resource reasons.
286	- Main may not classify bytes as receive result, task result, SCOE command success, report material, or northbound delivery status.
287	- Connection may normalize transport-level data availability and transport stats; it does not parse frames or define business result.
288	- Receive consumes byte batches, not raw unbounded per-packet renderer events.
289	- UI consumes throttled snapshots and read-only selectors; UI does not subscribe to raw serial/network events.
290	- Backpressure and overflow policy must be declared in future connection / receive design; this document does not set queue depth, window size, sampling rate or timeout thresholds.
291	- High-speed storage short-circuit is a boundary exception candidate. It must state whether data bypasses normal receive/display/trigger, which feature owns the decision, and what runtime/hardware evidence is required.
292	- Transport-level counters must not become global business statistics. Business stats belong to receive/send/task/SCOE/result/status owners as appropriate.
293	
294	## 9. Disconnect, reconnect, errors, and lifecycle events
295	
296	Principles:
297	
298	- Disconnect and error facts are transport-level facts owned by connection.
299	- Automatic reconnect is not assumed. If added, it must be explicit connection behavior with retry policy, cancellation, UI visibility and validation plan.
300	- A failed send should not itself decide task lifecycle. send may report transport failure; task decides task lifecycle through task-owned rules.
301	- A transport error should not define result/report/northbound failure semantics. result/report/northbound may consume transport facts through public projections.
302	- Close, cleanup and app shutdown must be idempotent and observable enough for runtime/manual validation.
303	- Connection lifecycle events should be low-frequency read model updates; high-frequency data flow should use batch/delta channels separately.
304	
305	Legacy evidence guard:
306	
307	- 旧网络 connect 对已有连接采取先 disconnect 再重建，未体现自动 retry 策略。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:33`
308	- 旧 TCP client 的 `error/close/timeout` 主要是状态更新和清理。`src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:120`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:128`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:132`
309	- 旧任务发送失败后才查询 target availability 并暂停任务。`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskExecutor.ts:229`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskExecutor.ts:233`
310	
311	## 10. Cross-feature communication
312	
313	Allowed patterns:
314	
315	- Public service: send, SCOE, task or pages call connection-owned command/query capabilities through explicit public entry.
316	- Runtime orchestration: runtime coordinates connection + receive or connection + send/SCOE when a flow crosses feature boundaries.
317	- Explicit event: connection emits declared transport-level events for receive/status/runtime consumers when async decoupling or batching is needed.
318	- Read-only selector: status/pages/widgets consume immutable snapshots or view models.
319	
320	Prohibited patterns:
321	
322	- receive / send / task / SCOE import connection internal `state`.
323	- receive / send / task / SCOE import connection internal adapter or private service implementation.
324	- connection imports receive/send/task/SCOE internal state to push business side effects.
325	- connection writes task status, send stats, receive parse result, SCOE command record, result truth, report file state or northbound transaction.
326	- pages/widgets subscribe to raw platform transport event streams.
327	- status reads raw connection internals instead of public snapshot.
328	
329	Feature consumption boundaries:
330	
331	| Consumer | May consume from connection | Must not do |
332	| --- | --- | --- |
333	| `receive` | incoming byte batches, source snapshot, lifecycle/error events relevant to input availability | import connection state, decide target availability, own transport lifecycle |
334	| `send` | target availability, transport write capability, transport-level write failure | own connection lifecycle, hardcode SCOE/northbound targets, define task result |
335	| `task` | target availability snapshot, explicit send/transport failure material via send result | import connection state, decide low-level retry by reading raw transport |
336	| `scoe` | declared fixed source/target availability through runtime exception, transport write capability | hardcode generic send internals, make connection own SCOE semantics |
337	| `status` | read-only connection summary and lifecycle events | own transport truth or raw platform resources |
338	| `northbound` | only internal projection if later needed through status/task/result/report; no direct transport target = deviceId mapping | treat serial/network target as customer device identity |
339	
340	## 11. Validation plan
341	
342	### 11.1 Vitest fixture can test
343	
344	- Transport config validation as pure data rules once feature design defines the model.
345	- Connection lifecycle state transitions without real I/O.
346	- Target route and target availability selector behavior, including serial target, TCP target, TCP server target and UDP remote host route as fixture cases.
347	- Error normalization categories without freezing final error codes.
348	- Runtime-independent connection read model updates from fake platform events.
349	- Cross-feature contract tests that assert receive/send/task/SCOE only use connection public boundary and not internal state.
350	- High-frequency batching logic in pure functions where queue/window/overflow policy has been designed.
351	
352	Limit:
353	
354	- Fixture cannot prove real serial/TCP/UDP device behavior, OS socket timing, driver behavior, hardware disconnect order, throughput or packaged app behavior.
355	
356	### 11.2 Fake transport adapter can test
357	
358	- Fake serial open/close/write/data event ordering.
359	- Fake TCP client connect/error/close/timeout and incoming data batches.
360	- Fake TCP server listen/client connect/client close/broadcast write behavior as transport-level facts.
361	- Fake UDP bind/send/message/remote host routing behavior.
362	- Failure injection: write failure, disconnect during write, duplicate close, stale connection event, listener cleanup, reconnect attempt cancellation.
363	- Backpressure and overflow policy simulations after future design defines parameters.
364	- Runtime wiring with connection service without touching Electron main/preload or real sockets.
365	
366	Limit:
367	
368	- Fake adapter does not replace main/preload bridge validation or hardware/runtime validation.
369	
370	### 11.3 Manual checklist must test
371	
372	- Connection page entry remains reachable.
373	- User can create, edit, select and delete serial/TCP/TCP server/UDP connection configurations once UI is implemented.
374	- Connect/disconnect button states and disabled states are coherent.
375	- Connection status, error message and last activity display are understandable.
376	- Manual recovery after failed connect, duplicate connect, disconnect and reconnect is visible.
377	- Serial and network test tools or equivalent visible workflows are available if preserved in scope.
378	- Target selection for send/SCOE is visible without exposing raw internal state.
379	- UI snapshot does not visibly lag, flicker or grow unbounded under ordinary data flow.
380	
381	Limit:
382	
383	- Manual checklist proves user-visible workflow only; it does not prove hardware throughput, packet order, backpressure or customer delivery.
384	
385	### 11.4 Runtime / hardware validation must test
386	
387	- Real serial enumerate/open/close/reopen, send, receive, error and disconnect order.
388	- Serial behavior across Windows target environment, port path changes, unavailable port and device unplug.
389	- TCP client real connect/send/receive/close/error/timeout.
390	- TCP server listen/client accept/multiple client send/close/server close cleanup.
391	- UDP bind/send/receive/broadcast option and remote host routing.
392	- High-frequency serial/TCP/UDP data with batching, queue, overflow, UI snapshot and no unbounded renderer event storm.
393	- Disconnect during high-frequency data and during write.
394	- Reconnect policy if implemented, including cancellation and operator-visible status.
395	- Main/preload/platform bridge behavior in Electron runtime, not only unit tests.
396	- Packaged data path only when storage/report/northbound flows require it.
397	- SCOE fixed source/target with real device only in the later SCOE feature validation.
398	
399	Cannot claim without evidence:
400	
401	- Real serial/TCP/UDP hardware chain complete.
402	- High-frequency steady-state behavior complete.
403	- SCOE device chain complete.
404	- Packaged data path complete.
405	- HTTP/FTP / northbound delivery complete.
406	- Customer closure complete.
407	
408	## 12. Deferred / blockers
409	
410	Deferred:
411	
412	- Concrete platform/preload/main channel names, methods, DTOs, errors and enums.
413	- Concrete connection config model, target id format and persistent storage shape.
414	- Queue depth, batch window, overflow/drop policy, retry/backoff policy and UI refresh interval.
415	- Whether TCP transport should preserve old `\r\n` splitting, move it into receive, or make it a configurable receive input rule.
416	- Whether old high-speed storage short-circuit is preserved, changed or split into storage/receive/runtime exception.
417	- Whether UDP remote host string serialization supports IPv6 or must use a different route representation.
418	- Whether old UI `websocket` option is dead legacy, candidate drop, or future extension; current old network type does not implement it.
419	- How network status-change drift is resolved; old renderer listens to status-change while main evidence primarily shows connection event and data event.
420	- SCOE fixed source/target exception design.
421	- Northbound `deviceId` and serial/network target separation in customer-facing flows.
422	
423	Blockers for implementation:
424	
425	- No accepted connection feature design yet.
426	- No accepted platform transport bridge capability map for serial/TCP/TCP server/UDP.
427	- No runtime evidence for real serial/TCP/UDP device behavior.
428	- No high-frequency performance evidence or target throughput.
429	- No hardware validation environment recorded for SCOE.
430	- No customer materials for northbound result/report/file delivery closure.
431	
432	Blockers for acceptance:
433	
434	- Missing real serial/TCP/UDP validation blocks claiming hardware connection accepted.
435	- Missing runtime high-frequency evidence blocks claiming buffer/batch/backpressure accepted.
436	- Missing packaged app evidence blocks claiming packaged data path accepted.
437	- Missing SCOE device evidence blocks claiming SCOE fixed source/target accepted.
438	- Missing customer schema/environment blocks claiming northbound delivery accepted.
439	
440	## 13. Carry-forward gates
441	
442	Before `connection` feature design:
443	
444	- Use this document as direct contract together with target structure, system architecture, platform API surface reduction, quality rules and review checklist.
445	- Fill legacy observable behavior ledger for serial/TCP/TCP server/UDP visible behavior.
446	- Decide connection owner / not owner for any target route or transport event.
447	- Declare public API necessity by capability category only.
448	- Declare state owner for static config, runtime facts, read model and UI snapshot.
449	- Declare runtime involvement and exception records.
450	- Declare fixture, fake adapter, manual, runtime and hardware validation scope.
451	
452	Before `receive` design:
453	
454	- Decide how receive consumes connection byte batches without importing connection internal state.
455	- Decide whether TCP line splitting is transport fact, receive input normalization, or deferred compatibility.
456	- Decide high-speed storage short-circuit boundary with storage and runtime.
457	
458	Before `send` design:
459	
460	- Decide how send asks connection to write bytes and how send owns business send result.
461	- Keep target availability read-only and separate from task lifecycle.
462	
463	Before `task` design:
464	
465	- Keep transport failure as input material, not task truth.
466	- Do not inherit old `send failure -> query target -> pause` as target architecture without explicit task design.
467	
468	Before `SCOE` design:
469	
470	- Register fixed TCP server / UDP source and target as boundary exception.
471	- Keep SCOE command semantics in `scoe`, not connection/send/receive.
472	
473	Before `northbound` design:
474	
475	- Keep serial/network transport target separate from customer device identity.
476	- Do not map connection status directly to external status without status/northbound projection design.
477	
```

15	
16	# Rewrite platform API surface reduction
17	
18	## 1. Scope
19	
20	Direct contract:
21	
22	- `AGENTS.md`
23	- `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-execution-charter.md`
24	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
25	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md`
26	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-boundaries.md`
27	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md`
28	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-shared-tooling-audit-plan.md`
29	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-pre-design-gate-and-sequencing.md`
30	- `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
31	- `codestable/quality/rewrite-review-checklist.md`
32	
33	Boundary guards:
34	
35	- 当前 `src`、`src-electron` 和 `public` 旧代码事实只作为 evidence、oracle 候选和迁移风险。
36	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md` 仍是 canonical 架构基线。
37	- 本轮不写业务代码，不移动代码，不定义最终 preload/main/renderer API schema。
38	- 本轮不写字段 schema、事件 payload、接口签名、northbound 字段、枚举或错误码。
39	- 本轮不进入 receive / send / task / SCOE / result / report / northbound 内部流程设计。
40	
41	Lane judgement:
42	
43	- 本轮属于 Lane B 的 global foundation design。
44	- 当前产物是后续 app shell、shared tooling 和业务 feature design 的前置 gate，不是任何业务 feature 的详细设计。
45	
46	Non-goals:
47	
48	- 不把旧 `src/api/common/*` 一一迁到 `rewrite/src/platform/*`。
49	- 不为每个旧 preload API 生成对应 platform facade。
50	- 不保留旧 `window.electron` 大包暴露方式。
51	- 不用 main process 的旧业务化 handler 证明目标 main 职责。
52	
53	Canonical decision:
54	
55	- `rewrite/src/platform` 是 renderer 访问桌面能力的唯一正式入口，但 platform 只表达桌面能力，不承载业务语义。
56	- `rewrite/` 是新的独立应用根目录；旧根配置只能作为 infra evidence，进入 `rewrite/` 前必须重新收缩 Quasar/Electron entry、boot、public/extraResources、lint/test scope 和 auto-import 白名单。
57	- preload 只暴露最小 typed bridge，不暴露裸 `invoke/send/on`、任意 channel 或大包能力对象。
58	- main 只保留平台资源、生命周期、受控文件/路径/对话框、串口/网络 I/O、HTTP/FTP transport primitive，以及必要的高频缓冲、批处理、队列、节流和背压。
59	- receive/send/task/SCOE/storage/result/report/northbound 的领域判断必须归口到可测试 TypeScript feature core/service 或 runtime 编排边界。
60	
61	Platform acceptance in this document only accepts capability category, prohibited surface, owner, validation/blocker, and runtime boundary exception registry entry when needed. It does not accept final API schema, interface signature, event payload, field enum, or preload/main/renderer contract details.
62	
63	## 2. Evidence summary
64	
65	Facts:
66	
67	- `src-electron/preload/index.ts` 通过 `contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('electron', api)` 暴露单一 `window.electron` 大包。
68	- `src-electron/preload/api/index.ts` 聚合 `window / menu / autoLaunch / serial / network / files / dataStorage / path / receive / highSpeedStorage / historyData / timerManager`。
69	- `src/api/common/*Api.ts` 基本按 preload 模块同构包装 `window.electron`，形成 renderer 侧旧平台大包兼容层。
70	- renderer 树下仍有直接 Electron/Node 依赖：`src/utils/common/ipcUtils.ts` import `ipcMain`，`src/utils/common/fileUtils.ts` import `fs/path`，`src/utils/common/dialogUtils.ts` import Electron dialog、BrowserWindow、fs、path，`src/composables/common/useFileDialog.ts` import `path` 并直连 `window.electron.files`。
71	- `src/composables/window/useWindowControls.ts` 直接读取 `window.electron.window`，绕过 `src/api/common/systemApi.ts` 中已有的 window wrapper。
72	- `src-electron/main/ipc/receiveHandlers.ts` 和 `receiveConfigCache.ts` 在 main 中执行 receive matching、mapping validation、data processing、cache/status 和统计构建，是 main 业务化风险证据。
73	- `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts` 在高频存储规则命中后短路普通 `network:data` 事件，是必须显式登记的高频边界例外候选。
74	- `src-electron/main/ipc/highSpeedStorageHandlers.ts` 同时包含文件流/轮转和 rule matching、connection id 解析、统计含义，混合了平台性能处理与 storage/receive 语义。
75	- 文件对话路径重复：`src/utils/common/fileDialogManager.ts`、`src/layouts/useFileDialog.ts`、`src/components/common/FileListDialog.vue`、`src/composables/common/useFileDialog.ts`、`src/utils/common/dialogUtils.ts` 并行存在。
76	- `timerManager` 从 main 到 preload 到 renderer wrapper 到 composable 已形成调度链，但 `onCustomEvent` 允许任意事件名订阅，存在把 timer 变成跨进程 event bus 的风险。
77	
78	Inference:
79	
80	- 现有三段式 Electron 链路可作为能力清单 evidence，但不能作为目标 API 组织。
81	- platform API surface 的主要目标是缩小、合并和退回 feature，而不是补齐所有旧 wrapper。
82	- `receiveApi`、`dataStorageApi`、`historyDataApi`、`highSpeedStorageApi` 是业务语义穿过 Electron 边界的重点风险。
83	- file/path/dialog/window 能力可先进入 app shell / shared tooling 设计，但只能按能力类别收口，不能保留多套 fallback。
84	- serial/network 只能作为 transport primitive 和平台资源生命周期进入 platform；connection status、target route、device identity、业务解析和结果语义不归 platform。
85	
86	## 3. Platform API Surface Reduction Decision Table
87	
88	Legend:
89	
90	- `Evidence only`: 旧 API 是否只能作为迁移证据或 oracle 候选。
91	- `Blocked by`: 后续继续推进时需要等待的 design、runtime、hardware、customer 或 packaged evidence。
92	
93	| Old capability / file | Current responsibility | Observed risk | Target decision | Reason | What renderer may access | What preload may expose | What main may retain | What must move back to feature | Evidence only | Blocked by |
94	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
95	| `src/api/common/index.ts` | 汇总所有旧 renderer wrapper | 大包聚合复制 `window.electron` 能力面 | delete / merge | 新 platform 不按旧文件名平移，不保留万能 barrel | 只访问按能力收敛的 `rewrite/src/platform` facade | none | none | none | yes | platform design acceptance |
96	| `src/api/common/systemApi.ts` | window/menu/autoLaunch 兼容 wrapper 和 fallback | `electronAPI` 兜底掩盖能力缺失；autoLaunch 无调用证据 | merge / platform facade / deferred | window/menu 属 app shell/platform；autoLaunch 属高权限系统设置，需显式需求 | app shell 通过 platform 使用最小 window/menu 能力 | typed shell bridge，autoLaunch 后置 | window/menu 平台操作；autoLaunch 若被确认才保留系统调用 | 设置语义、用户授权语义回 `settings/app` | partial | app shell design；autoLaunch 用户需求 |
97	| `src/api/common/filesApi.ts` | 文件元数据、目录、JSON/text read/write/delete | 泛路径、泛文件读写和业务 JSON import/export 混在一起 | merge / platform facade | 文件能力是真平台能力，但必须受控并和业务语义分离 | feature adapter 或 app shell file dialog 通过 platform 访问受控 file capability | typed bounded file/dialog bridge | 受控文件 I/O、目录准备、元数据读取 | frame/storage/report import/export 语义、JSON 内容语义 | partial | storage/app shell design；packaged path evidence |
