120	| `src-electron/preload/api/timerManager.ts` | scheduling bridge with tick/custom event | arbitrary event subscription | platform scheduling / merge | scheduling 可以平台化，但不承载业务 channel | feature consumer via scheduling facade | typed scheduling bridge; no arbitrary event channel | timer lifecycle/cleanup | task/SCOE/status timer purpose | partial | scheduling gate |
121	| `src-electron/main/ipc/windowHandlers.ts`, `menuHandlers.ts` | window/menu operations | 低风险平台能力，但 menu 无上游调用证据 | preload-main implementation / deferred for menu | 保留最小 shell operations，未用能力后置 | none direct | none | minimal window/menu operations | none | partial | app shell/menu decision |
122	| `src-electron/main/ipc/serialHandlers.ts` | serial resource, data/status events, cleanup | 高频逐包事件和状态广播可能直接冲击 stores | preload-main implementation + high-frequency exception | serial I/O 属 main；业务分类不属 main | none direct | none | port lifecycle、I/O、cleanup、buffer/batch/backpressure | receive parsing、task/result/status meaning | yes | hardware/runtime; event model |
123	| `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts` | TCP/UDP lifecycle/data/status，高速存储短路 | `shouldStore` 业务分流藏在 transport handler | preload-main implementation + runtime exception | socket I/O 可保留；short-circuit 必须登记并迁出语义 | none direct | none | socket lifecycle、I/O、buffer/batch/backpressure | high-speed rule、receive classification、device identity | yes | high-speed/connection design |
124	| `src-electron/main/ipc/receiveHandlers.ts`, `receiveConfigCache.ts` | receive matching、processing、cache、stats | main 承载 receive 业务核 | feature-owned / delete from main / exception only if temporary | main 不拥有 receive 解析或 cache 语义 | none | none | only platform buffer if registered | matching、mapping、cache、stats、result output | yes | receive design |
125	| `src-electron/main/ipc/dataStorageHandlers.ts`, `fileMetadataHandlers.ts` | JSON storage CRUD、file metadata/I/O | data type CRUD 和业务路径 map 与平台 I/O 混在一起 | split platform file I/O + feature-owned storage | file I/O 留 main；storage 语义回 feature | none direct | none | bounded file operations、metadata、directory prep | data type lifecycle、serialization/import/export | yes | storage local baseline |
126	| `src-electron/main/ipc/historyDataHandlers.ts` | history append/load/compress/cleanup/CSV export | history schema、CSV 字段和清理策略是业务语义 | split + feature-owned / deferred | main 可执行文件流/压缩；history 语义归 storage | none direct | none | file/stream/compress executor | history model、CSV/report material semantics | yes | storage/report; packaged runtime |
127	| `src-electron/main/ipc/highSpeedStorageHandlers.ts` | stream/rotation/rule matching/stats | platform perf 和业务规则混合 | preload-main implementation + feature-owned + runtime exception | 保留高频文件设施，业务规则必须登记 owner | none direct | none | stream、rotation、queue、backpressure | rule matching、short-circuit、stats meaning | yes | high-speed design；perf/hardware |
128	| `src-electron/main/ipc/timerManagerHandlers.ts` | main timers、tick event、cleanup | 容易成为业务事件总线或 hidden scheduler | preload-main implementation + scheduling facade | main timer 可规避 renderer throttling；feature owns timer meaning | none direct | none | timer lifecycle、cleanup、drift observation | task/SCOE/status semantics | partial | timer drift/runtime |
129	| `src-electron/main/ipc/index.ts` | 一次性注册所有 handlers | 聚合业务 handler 强化旧边界 | merge into platform main tooling / deferred | main registry 可保留，但按 accepted capability 注册 | none | none | capability registry | feature business handlers | yes | platform/main boundary |
130	
131	## 4. Minimal platform capability map
132	
133	本文只写能力类别，不写接口 schema。
134	
135	| Capability category | Why renderer cannot do it directly | Platform owns | Platform must not own | Features consume it | Validation required | Reduction rule |
136	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
137	| window / menu / app shell | renderer 不能直接访问 Electron window/menu/app lifecycle | 最小窗口控制、菜单显示状态、app shell 需要的系统能力；autoLaunch 仅在需求确认后进入 | 页面业务流程、任务生命周期、状态事实、用户业务语义 | `app` shell、`pages` shell、`settings` if visible config is confirmed | manual + runtime | 只保留 app shell 真正使用的 shell capability；无调用证据的 menu/autoLaunch 后置或删除 |
138	| files / path / dialogs | renderer 不能直接访问 `fs/path` 或 native dialog；打包态路径需 Electron/main 判断 | 受控文件选择、受控读写、目录准备、data path、packaged state、路径类别 | frame import/export 语义、storage schema、history/CSV/report 语义、northbound delivery closure | `frame`、`storage`、`settings`、`report`、app shell file dialog | fixture for adapters; manual for dialogs; runtime for packaged data path | 合并多套 file dialog 和 path fallback；禁止 raw path resolver 和任意路径读写成为业务 API |
139	| serial / network transport | renderer 不能访问 `serialport`、socket、`net`，也不应承接 I/O 生命周期 | 端口/连接资源、打开/关闭、字节收发、连接事件、必要 buffering/batch/backpressure hook | frame matching、receive parsing、send result、task state、SCOE completion、northbound device identity | `connection` first; then `receive`、`send`、`scoe`、`status` | runtime + hardware for serial/TCP/UDP | 只保留 transport primitive；connection owns status/target model，feature owns business interpretation |
140	| scheduling | renderer timer 可能被节流；部分长周期/高频 timer 需要主进程稳定调度 | timer lifecycle、tick delivery、cleanup、drift observation | timed send 语义、task state machine、SCOE wait/completion、status polling meaning | `task`、`scoe`、`status`、app lifecycle where justified | fixture for cancellation model; runtime/manual for drift | 收缩为 scheduling facility；禁止任意 channel 和万能跨进程 event bus |
141	| packaged data path | userData、resources、权限和长期写入只有打包态可确认 | packaged state、受控数据根、目录准备、路径类别 | 旧 data path 常量、storage schema、report naming、file retention semantics | `storage`、`settings`、`report`、`northbound` when delivery needs local files | packaged runtime validation | 只定义 capability category；具体路径策略等 storage/app package evidence |
142	| optional HTTP/FTP transport | renderer 不应持有 FTP/HTTP 交付资源和失败恢复；外部交付需平台 transport | HTTP/FTP transport primitive、连接生命周期、文件上传 I/O if confirmed | northbound transaction semantics、external error/refusal mapping、TestReport schema、delivery success meaning | `northbound` via platform, using `report/storage` materials | customer + runtime validation | 只作为 optional transport 能力保留；无 customer evidence 前不实现或冻结 contract |
143	| high-frequency buffer / batch / backpressure exception | 高频串口/网络/文件链路不能逐包无节制穿透 renderer 和页面 | 与平台资源绑定的 buffer、batch、queue、throttle、backpressure、file stream/rotation | receive classification、storage rule meaning、task/result/status/report semantics | `connection`、`receive`、`storage`、`scoe` | fixture for small rules; runtime/hardware/perf validation for chain | 每个高频例外必须登记；main 只能保留性能侧处理，业务语义回 feature |
144	
145	## 5. API surface shrink list
146	
147	### 5.1 Delete
148	
149	- 删除目标架构中的 `window.electron` 大包暴露方式；旧 `src-electron/preload/index.ts` 只能作为证据。
150	- 删除或停止扩展 `src/api/common/index.ts` 这类万能 renderer API barrel。
151	- 删除 renderer 侧直接 Electron/Node 入口：`src/utils/common/ipcUtils.ts`、`src/utils/common/fileUtils.ts`、`src/utils/common/dialogUtils.ts` 的当前归口形态不能进入新 renderer 树。
152	- 删除 `timerManager.onCustomEvent` 这类任意 channel 订阅能力；后续如需事件，只能由 runtime/feature design 声明固定边界。
153	- 删除 `src/composables/common/useFileDialog.ts` 与主 file dialog flow 重复的直连/fallback 路径。
154	- 删除 `systemApi.electronAPI` 这种兼容兜底对象在目标架构中的正式地位。
155	
156	### 5.2 Merge
157	
158	- 合并 window/menu/app shell 能力：app shell 只消费一条 platform shell capability path，不再让 `useWindowControls` 和 `systemApi.windowAPI` 并行。
159	- 合并 files/path/dialog 能力：保留一条 app shell file dialog + platform file/path + feature adapter 路径，不保留 `dialogUtils`、`fileDialogManager`、`useFileDialog` 多套 fallback。
160	- 合并 file/path 的平台实现：platform 只表达受控文件、受控路径和打包态能力；storage/report/frame/settings 各自拥有业务语义。
161	- 合并 scheduling 能力：main timer、preload timer、renderer wrapper 和 composable 统一为 platform scheduling facility + feature consumer，不复制局部 timer manager。
162	- 合并 IPC tooling 到 main platform tooling；renderer `src/utils` 不再持有 `ipcMain` helper。
163	
164	### 5.3 Migration evidence only
165	
166	- `src/api/common/*Api.ts` 是旧能力清单和 fallback 行为证据，不是新 platform 命名或接口合同。
167	- `src-electron/preload/api/*` 是旧 bridge 形态证据，不是目标 preload 暴露清单。
168	- `src-electron/main/ipc/*` 是平台资源、业务泄漏和高频风险证据，不是目标 main 责任边界。
169	- 旧 `path.getDataPath()` 指向开发/打包路径的行为只能作为 packaged data path 风险证据，不能冻结为长期路径策略。
170	- 旧 high-speed storage short-circuit 是必须解释的行为证据，不能默认成为新 receive/storage 数据流。
171	
172	### 5.4 Feature-owned
173	
174	- `receiveApi` 不能迁成 `platform/receive`。receive matching、mapping、cache、stats、result output 归 `features/receive`。
175	- `dataStorageApi`、`historyDataApi`、`highSpeedStorageApi` 不能都原样迁成 platform API。storage/history/high-speed 的数据模型、清理口径、导出语义、rule matching 和统计含义归 `features/storage` 或对应 feature design。
176	- serial/network 的 connection status、target route、target availability 和 transport identity 归 `features/connection`；northbound device identity 归 `features/northbound`。
177	- timerManager 的 timed send、task lifecycle、SCOE wait/completion、status polling meaning 分别归 `task`、`scoe`、`status`，不归 platform scheduling。
178	- file import/export validation、旧 JSON migration、history/CSV/report material、report generation 和 northbound delivery closure 都归 feature。
179	
180	### 5.5 Deferred
181	
182	- `autoLaunch` 后置到 settings/app shell 需求确认；无用户可见需求前不作为目标 platform surface。
183	- `menuAPI` 后置到 app shell/menu UX 确认；当前无上游调用证据时不主动暴露。
184	- raw `path.resolve` 与最终 `getDataPath` 语义后置到 packaged data path design 和 runtime evidence。
185	- serial/TCP/UDP 事件模型、batch/backpressure 参数、queue/window/overflow 策略后置到 connection/receive design 和 runtime evidence。
186	- HTTP/FTP transport 后置到 northbound phase-one/customer assets；本轮只保留 optional platform capability category。
187	- high-speed storage short-circuit 是否保留、何时短路普通 receive/display/trigger 链，后置到 storage/receive/high-frequency design。
188	
189	### 5.6 Runtime exception registry required
190	
191	后续触达以下能力时必须登记 runtime boundary exception，而不是隐式沉积在 platform/main handler 中：
192	
193	- high-frequency buffer/batch/backpressure for serial/network data.
194	- high-speed storage short-circuit or direct file write path.
195	- packaged data path and writable data root.
196	- temporary main-side receive processing if any migration step cannot remove it immediately.
197	- SCOE fixed source/target/completion boundary.
198	- optional HTTP/FTP northbound delivery transport.
199	- scheduling assumptions that rely on main timers, multi-window target, drift control, or cleanup ordering.
200	
201	## 6. Special reduction decisions
202	
203	- `receiveApi` 不能迁成 `platform/receive`。receive 是 feature-owned domain; platform 只提供 transport input、必要 batch/backpressure 和 typed boundary。
204	- `dataStorageApi/historyDataApi/highSpeedStorageApi` 不能都原样迁成 platform API。它们必须拆成 platform file/path/high-frequency facility 与 storage/history/high-speed feature semantics。
205	- `timerManagerApi` 不能变成万能跨进程调度中心。platform scheduling 只负责 timer facility；feature 必须声明用途、取消策略、owner 和验证。
206	- `serialApi/networkApi` 只能保留 transport 能力。connection/status/target/device identity 语义不归 platform；northbound `deviceId` 不等同 serial/network target。
207	- file/path/dialog/window 能力必须合并重复入口，删除静默 fallback 和绕行路径，不保留多套并行 file dialog 或 window control path。
208	
209	## 7. Runtime Boundary Exception Registry Template
210	
211	本文只定义登记格式，不登记具体实现。后续任何 exception record 必须按此模板补齐。
212	
213	| Field | Required content | Must not contain |
214	| --- | --- | --- |
215	| Exception name | 稳定、可检索的例外名称 | 临时实现细节或旧 handler 文件名本身 |
216	| Why renderer/feature core cannot own it directly | 说明为什么不能由 renderer 或 feature core 直接承载，例如平台资源、权限、性能、打包态、硬件链路 | “减少 IPC” 这类不足以成立的理由 |
217	| Platform/main retained responsibility | main/platform 只保留哪些平台侧或性能侧处理 | 业务运算、协议语义、任务推进、报告语义、northbound 错误转换 |
218	| Business owner feature | 明确业务语义最终归属哪个 feature | `platform` 或 `runtime` 作为业务 owner |
219	| Data flow boundary | 输入来自哪里、平台侧处理到哪里结束、feature 从哪里接管；只写边界，不写 payload schema | 字段 schema、接口签名、事件 payload |
220	| Validation required | fixture/manual/runtime/hardware/customer/package 哪些层级必须覆盖 | 用 build/lint 替代 runtime/hardware/customer evidence |
221	| Exit condition / future simplification possibility | 何时可以移除例外、下沉到 feature core、或缩小 main 保留职责 | 无期限保留或“以后再看” |
222	
223	Template rules:
224	
225	- 每个例外都必须能回答：为什么不能由可测试 TypeScript core 直接拥有，main 到底只保留了什么，业务语义在哪里归口，如何验证，何时退出。
226	- 例外登记只允许扩大审计可见性，不允许把业务规则合法化到 main。
227	- 没有登记的 high-frequency、SCOE、packaged data path、HTTP/FTP 或 main-side business processing，后续审查应判为 `revise-required`。
228	
229	## 8. Follow-up gate
230	
231	### 8.1 Platform abilities that can enter shared-tooling-app-shell-ownership next
232	
233	| Ability | Why allowed now | Guard |
234	| --- | --- | --- |
235	| window controls | app shell 需要，能力低频且边界清楚 | 只设计 app shell consumer 和 platform category，不写最终 API schema |
236	| menu visibility | 可作为 app shell candidate | 当前无上游调用证据，默认 deferred until UX need |
237	| file dialog shell | 重复路径明显，适合先做 owner 归口 | app shell owns dialog flow; feature owns import/export semantics |
238	| bounded files/path category | storage/frame/settings 都依赖，但可先定禁止面 | 不定义具体 path schema；packaged path 需 runtime evidence |
239	| scheduling category | timer facility 需要先防止业务化 | 不定义 task/SCOE/status timer semantics |
240	| renderer direct access cleanup rules | 与 shared/tooling 归口直接相关 | 不做代码迁移，只写 owner/delete/merge 判断 |
241	
242	### 8.2 Must wait for connection / receive / storage / SCOE / northbound design
243	
244	| Ability | Must wait for | Reason |
245	| --- | --- | --- |
246	| serial/network event model and target status | `connection` design | transport target、status、availability 和 batch boundary 需要 connection owner |
247	| receive processing/cache replacement | `receive` design | parser、mapping、result、stats 都是 receive semantics |
248	| high-speed storage rule and short-circuit behavior | `storage` + `receive` + high-frequency design | 关系到是否进入普通 receive/display/trigger 链 |
249	| local storage/history/CSV semantics | `storage-local-baseline` | data model、cleanup、export 和 migration 属 storage |
250	| SCOE fixed source/target/completion | `scoe` design | 属领域例外，不可塞进 generic transport/receive/send |
251	| HTTP/FTP transport usage | `northbound` + customer materials | transport primitive 可保留，使用语义必须等甲方闭环 |
252	| result/report delivery interaction | `result` + `report` + `northbound` | result truth、report generation、delivery closure 必须分开 |
253	
254	### 8.3 Blocks implementation but not boundary design
255	
256	| Blocker | Blocks implementation of | Boundary design may still decide |
257	| --- | --- | --- |
258	| Real serial/TCP/UDP evidence missing | transport adapter, send/receive real I/O | owner, capability category, validation plan |
259	| High-frequency batch/backpressure parameters missing | concrete queue/window/overflow implementation | principle, exception template, feature owner |
260	| Packaged data path unverified | persistent path implementation | path owner, runtime validation requirement |
261	| Timer drift/multi-window target unverified | concrete scheduling adapter behavior | scheduling category and feature ownership split |
262	| SCOE hardware unavailable | real SCOE command path | SCOE owner and exception boundary |
263	| HTTP/FTP environment unavailable | northbound transport implementation | optional transport category and customer blocker |
264	
265	### 8.4 Blocks acceptance
266	
267	| Missing evidence | Cannot claim accepted for |
268	| --- | --- |
269	| No real serial/TCP/UDP validation | hardware connection, real send/receive order, high-frequency data behavior |
270	| No SCOE device validation | SCOE timeout, state loop, completion condition, fixed source/target correctness |
271	| No packaged app data path validation | packaged persistence, backup, cleanup, report material path |
272	| No runtime evidence for high-speed storage | short-circuit correctness, throughput, file rotation, stats consistency |
273	| No customer schema / enum / error / TestReport sample | northbound result/report/task/status customer closure |
274	| No HTTP/FTP environment and receiving-side confirmation | file delivery, report upload, completion notice, retry/failure compensation |
275	
276	### 8.5 Impact on first batch design
277	
278	| First batch candidate | Allowed after this document | Required guard |
279	| --- | --- | --- |
280	| `shared-tooling-app-shell-ownership` | yes | Must use this document as direct contract for platform/app shell shrink decisions; no code movement |
281	| `frame` | yes | File import/export uses platform/file category only as boundary; old JSON migration stays frame/storage design |
282	| `settings` | yes | System settings like autoLaunch remain deferred until explicit user-facing need |
283	| `storage-local-baseline` | yes, local-only | Must split storage semantics from platform file/path; packaged path marked runtime blocker |
284	| `connection` | not first until platform transport gate accepted | serial/network must be transport-only; target/device identity split required |
285	| `receive` | not first | Needs frame snapshot, connection input model, high-frequency boundary |
286	| `send` | not first | Needs frame/send asset boundary and connection target model |
287	| `task` | not first | Needs scheduling gate and receive/send outputs |
288	| `scoe` | not first | Needs exception registry and connection/receive/send boundaries |
289	| `result/report/northbound` | not first | Needs task/status/result/report boundaries plus customer materials for northbound |
290	
291	## 9. Review implications
292	
293	- If a future change recreates `window.electron` or another large renderer capability object, review outcome should be `revise-required`.
294	- If a future change exposes bare `invoke/send/on`, arbitrary channel subscription, or raw path/file operation to renderer, review outcome should be `revise-required`.
295	- If main retains receive/send/task/SCOE/result/report/northbound business semantics without an explicit exception record and owner/exit condition, review outcome should be `revise-required`.
296	- If platform facade names mirror every old `src/api/common/*Api.ts` file without shrink/merge/delete decisions, review outcome should be `revise-required`.
297	- If feature design touches platform/preload/main but does not list this document as direct contract or boundary guard, review outcome should be `blocked`.
298	
299	## 10. Next-step judgement
300	
301	Allowed:
302	
303	- Enter `shared-tooling-app-shell-ownership` as the next global foundation design.
304	- Enter `platform-app-shell-file-dialog` as the bridge gate that applies this platform shrink decision to file/path/dialog/window controls before frame/settings/storage implementation.
305	- Start first business feature designs for `frame`, `settings`, and local-only `storage-local-baseline`, provided they carry this document as direct contract or boundary guard when touching platform/file/path/dialog.
306	
307	Not allowed yet:
308	
309	- Do not start receive/send/task/SCOE/result/report/northbound implementation.
310	- Do not define final preload/main/renderer API schema from this document.
311	- Do not migrate old `src/api/common/*` files directly into `rewrite/src/platform/*`.
312	- Do not claim hardware, packaged runtime, high-speed storage, HTTP/FTP, or customer closure from static evidence.
313	
```

104	
105	            for (const entry of entries) {
106	              const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
107	
108	              if (entry.isDirectory()) {
109	                files.push(...(await findDesktopFiles(fullPath)));

111	                files.push(fullPath);
112	              }
113	            }
114	          } catch {
115	            return files;
116	          }
117	
118	          return files;
119	        }

125	
126	          if (!content.includes('Encoding=')) {
127	            content = content.replace('[Desktop Entry]', '[Desktop Entry]\nEncoding=UTF-8');
128	            await fs.writeFile(desktopFile, content, 'utf-8');
129	          }
130	        }
131	      },
132	    },
133	  },
134	}));

1	import { app, BrowserWindow } from 'electron';
2	import os from 'node:os';
3	import path from 'node:path';
4	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
5	
6	const platform = process.platform || os.platform();
7	const currentDir = fileURLToPath(new URL('.', import.meta.url));
8	
9	let mainWindow: BrowserWindow | undefined;
10	
11	function getPreloadPath() {
12	  const preloadFolder = process.env.QUASAR_ELECTRON_PRELOAD_FOLDER ?? 'preload';
13	  const preloadExtension = process.env.QUASAR_ELECTRON_PRELOAD_EXTENSION ?? '.cjs';
14	
15	  return path.resolve(currentDir, path.join(preloadFolder, `index${preloadExtension}`));
16	}
17	
18	async function createWindow() {
19	  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
20	    width: 1200,
21	    height: 800,
22	    minWidth: 960,
23	    minHeight: 640,
24	    useContentSize: true,
25	    show: false,
26	    webPreferences: {
27	      nodeIntegration: false,
28	      contextIsolation: true,
29	      sandbox: false,
30	      preload: getPreloadPath(),
31	    },
32	  });
33	
34	  const appUrl = process.env.APP_URL;
35	  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => {
36	    mainWindow?.show();
37	  });
38	
39	  mainWindow.webContents.once('did-finish-load', () => {
40	    if (mainWindow?.isVisible() === false) {
41	      mainWindow.show();
42	    }
43	  });
44	
45	  if (process.env.DEV && appUrl) {
46	    await mainWindow.loadURL(appUrl);
47	  } else {
48	    await mainWindow.loadFile('index.html');
49	  }
50	
51	  mainWindow.on('closed', () => {
52	    mainWindow = undefined;
53	  });
54	}
55	
56	void app.whenReady().then(createWindow);
57	
58	app.on('window-all-closed', () => {
59	  if (platform !== 'darwin') {
60	    app.quit();
61	  }
62	});
63	
64	app.on('activate', () => {
65	  if (mainWindow === undefined) {
66	    void createWindow();
67	  }
68	});
69

1	import { cloneTransportConfig, deriveTransportTarget, type TransportConfig } from '../core';
2	import type {
3	  ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome,
4	  ConnectionAdapterErrorInput,
5	  ConnectionAdapterEvent,
6	  ConnectionAdapterOperation,
7	  ConnectionTransportAdapter,
8	  TransportWriteRequest,
9	} from './ports';
10	
11	export interface FakeConnectionAdapterFailure {
12	  readonly operation: ConnectionAdapterOperation;
13	  readonly connectionId?: string;
14	  readonly error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput;
15	}
16	
17	export interface CreateFakeConnectionTransportAdapterOptions {
18	  readonly failures?: readonly FakeConnectionAdapterFailure[];
19	  readonly now?: () => string;
20	}
21	
22	export interface FakeConnectionTransportAdapter extends ConnectionTransportAdapter {
23	  setFailure(failure: FakeConnectionAdapterFailure): void;
24	  clearFailures(): void;
25	  pushData(connectionId: string, bytes: readonly number[]): void;
26	  pushError(connectionId: string, error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput): void;
27	  pushClose(connectionId: string): void;
28	  readStoredConfig(connectionId: string): TransportConfig | undefined;
29	  readWrittenBatches(connectionId: string): number[][];
30	}
31	
32	function defaultNow(): string {
33	  return new Date().toISOString();
34	}
35	
36	function cloneBytes(bytes: readonly number[]): number[] {
37	  return [...bytes];
38	}
39	
40	function findFailure(
41	  failures: readonly FakeConnectionAdapterFailure[],
42	  operation: ConnectionAdapterOperation,
43	  connectionId?: string,
44	): FakeConnectionAdapterFailure | undefined {
45	  return failures.find((failure) => {
46	    if (failure.operation !== operation) {
47	      return false;
48	    }
49	    return failure.connectionId === undefined || failure.connectionId === connectionId;
50	  });
51	}
52	
53	function rejected(
54	  failure: FakeConnectionAdapterFailure,
55	  connectionId: string,
56	  occurredAt: string,
57	): ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome {
58	  return {
59	    ok: false,
60	    error: failure.error,
61	    events: [
62	      {
63	        kind: 'error',
64	        connectionId,
65	        occurredAt,
66	        error: failure.error,
67	      },
68	    ],
69	  };
70	}
71	
72	export function createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter(
73	  options: CreateFakeConnectionTransportAdapterOptions = {},
74	): FakeConnectionTransportAdapter {
75	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
76	  const configs = new Map<string, TransportConfig>();
77	  const writes = new Map<string, number[][]>();
78	  const eventQueue: ConnectionAdapterEvent[] = [];
79	  let failures = [...(options.failures ?? [])];
80	
81	  return {
82	    async connect(config): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome> {
83	      const occurredAt = now();
84	      const failure = findFailure(failures, 'connect', config.id);
85	      if (failure) {
86	        return rejected(failure, config.id, occurredAt);
87	      }
88	
89	      const configCopy = cloneTransportConfig(config);
90	      configs.set(configCopy.id, configCopy);
91	      return {
92	        ok: true,
93	        events: [
94	          {
95	            kind: 'connected',
96	            connectionId: configCopy.id,
97	            occurredAt,
98	            target: deriveTransportTarget(configCopy, true),
99	          },
100	        ],
101	      };
102	    },
103	
104	    async disconnect(connectionId): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome> {
105	      const occurredAt = now();
106	      const failure = findFailure(failures, 'disconnect', connectionId);
107	      if (failure) {
108	        return rejected(failure, connectionId, occurredAt);
109	      }
110	
111	      const config = configs.get(connectionId);
112	      configs.delete(connectionId);
113	      return {
114	        ok: true,
115	        events: [
116	          {
117	            kind: 'disconnected',
118	            connectionId,
119	            occurredAt,
120	            ...(config ? { target: deriveTransportTarget(config, false) } : {}),
121	          },
122	        ],
123	      };
124	    },
125	
126	    async write(request: TransportWriteRequest): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome> {
127	      const occurredAt = now();
128	      const failure = findFailure(failures, 'write', request.connectionId);
129	      if (failure) {
130	        return {
131	          ok: false,
132	          error: failure.error,
133	          events: [
134	            {
135	              kind: 'write-failed',
136	              connectionId: request.connectionId,
137	              occurredAt,
138	              byteLength: request.bytes.length,
139	              error: failure.error,
140	            },
141	          ],
142	        };
143	      }
144	
145	      if (!configs.has(request.connectionId)) {
146	        const error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput = {
147	          kind: 'resource-unavailable',
148	          message: `Connection is not open: ${request.connectionId}.`,
149	          recoverable: true,
150	        };
151	        return {
152	          ok: false,
153	          error,
154	          events: [
155	            {
156	              kind: 'write-failed',
157	              connectionId: request.connectionId,
158	              occurredAt,
159	              byteLength: request.bytes.length,
160	              error,
161	            },
162	          ],
163	        };
164	      }
165	
166	      const existing = writes.get(request.connectionId) ?? [];
167	      writes.set(request.connectionId, [...existing, cloneBytes(request.bytes)]);
168	
169	      return {
170	        ok: true,
171	        events: [
172	          {
173	            kind: 'write-accepted',
174	            connectionId: request.connectionId,
175	            occurredAt,
176	            byteLength: request.bytes.length,
177	          },
178	        ],
179	      };
180	    },
181	
182	    async cleanup(): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome> {
183	      const occurredAt = now();
184	      const failure = findFailure(failures, 'cleanup');
185	      if (failure) {
186	        return rejected(failure, 'cleanup', occurredAt);
187	      }
188	
189	      const events = Array.from(configs.values()).map((config) => ({
190	        kind: 'disconnected' as const,
191	        connectionId: config.id,
192	        occurredAt,
193	        target: deriveTransportTarget(config, false),
194	      }));
195	      configs.clear();
196	      return { ok: true, events };
197	    },
198	
199	    async drainEvents(): Promise<readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[]> {
200	      return eventQueue.splice(0, eventQueue.length).map((event) => ({
201	        ...event,
202	        ...(event.bytes ? { bytes: cloneBytes(event.bytes) } : {}),
203	      }));
204	    },
205	
206	    setFailure(failure) {
207	      failures = [...failures, failure];
208	    },
209	
210	    clearFailures() {
211	      failures = [];
212	    },
213	
214	    pushData(connectionId, bytes) {
215	      eventQueue.push({
216	        kind: 'data',
217	        connectionId,
218	        occurredAt: now(),
219	        bytes: cloneBytes(bytes),
220	        byteLength: bytes.length,
221	      });
222	    },
223	
224	    pushError(connectionId, error) {
225	      eventQueue.push({
226	        kind: 'error',
227	        connectionId,
228	        occurredAt: now(),
229	        error,
230	      });
231	    },
232	
233	    pushClose(connectionId) {
234	      const config = configs.get(connectionId);
235	      configs.delete(connectionId);
236	      eventQueue.push({
237	        kind: 'disconnected',
238	        connectionId,
239	        occurredAt: now(),
240	        ...(config ? { target: deriveTransportTarget(config, false) } : {}),
241	      });
242	    },
243	
244	    readStoredConfig(connectionId) {
245	      const config = configs.get(connectionId);
246	      return config ? cloneTransportConfig(config) : undefined;
247	    },
248	
249	    readWrittenBatches(connectionId) {
250	      return (writes.get(connectionId) ?? []).map(cloneBytes);
251	    },
252	  };
253	}
254	
```

1	import type {
2	  TransportConfig,
3	  TransportErrorKind,
4	  TransportKind,
5	  TransportTargetSnapshot,
6	} from '../core';
7	
8	export type ConnectionAdapterOperation = 'connect' | 'disconnect' | 'write' | 'cleanup';
9	
10	export interface ConnectionAdapterErrorInput {
11	  readonly kind: TransportErrorKind;
12	  readonly message: string;
13	  readonly recoverable?: boolean;
14	}
15	
16	export type ConnectionAdapterEventKind =
17	  | 'connected'
18	  | 'disconnected'
19	  | 'data'
20	  | 'write-accepted'
21	  | 'write-failed'
22	  | 'error';
23	
24	export interface ConnectionAdapterEvent {
25	  readonly kind: ConnectionAdapterEventKind;
26	  readonly connectionId: string;
27	  readonly occurredAt?: string;
28	  readonly target?: TransportTargetSnapshot;
29	  readonly bytes?: readonly number[];
30	  readonly byteLength?: number;
31	  readonly error?: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput;
32	}
33	
34	export interface ConnectionAdapterAccepted {
35	  readonly ok: true;
36	  readonly events: readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[];
37	}
38	
39	export interface ConnectionAdapterRejected {
40	  readonly ok: false;
41	  readonly error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput;
42	  readonly events?: readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[];
43	}
44	
45	export type ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome =
46	  | ConnectionAdapterAccepted
47	  | ConnectionAdapterRejected;
48	
49	export interface TransportWriteRequest {
50	  readonly connectionId: string;
51	  readonly bytes: readonly number[];
52	  readonly targetId?: string;
53	}
54	
55	export interface ConnectionTransportAdapter {
56	  connect(config: TransportConfig): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
57	  disconnect(connectionId: string): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
58	  write(request: TransportWriteRequest): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
59	  cleanup(): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
60	  drainEvents(): Promise<readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[]>;
61	}
62	
63	export interface ConnectionResourceCandidate {
64	  readonly kind: TransportKind;
65	  readonly id: string;
66	  readonly label: string;
67	}
68

所有代码和配置已读完，测试通过。现在输出完整验证报告。

---

## 1. 直接合同 / 边界约束

**直接合同:**
- `codestable/features/rewrite-connection/rewrite-connection-bridge-implementation-design.md` (§9 native dependency risk, §11 gate criteria)
- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md` (§12 implementation gate)
- `AGENTS.md` (Electron/platform boundary)
- 当前代码：`rewrite/src/features/connection`、`rewrite/src/platform`、`rewrite/src-electron`、`rewrite/quasar.config.ts`、`rewrite/package.json`

**边界约束:**
- 只做前置准备验证，不改 `rewrite/src/features/` 下任何业务代码
- main 不解析帧、不匹配接收、不推进任务状态、不解释 SCOE
- preload 只暴露类型化桥接
- renderer 不直接访问 serialport / Node / IPC

---

## 2. 门禁清单

### G1: serialport 版本与 Electron 兼容性

**状态: 待处理 — 需要安装验证**

| 项目 | 当前 | 需要 | 证据 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Electron 版本 | 35.1.3 | 不变 | `rewrite/package.json` |
| Node 版本 | v22.21.1 | >=22 <23 | 运行时确认 |
| 旧 serialport | 10.5.0 | 需升级到 12.x | 旧 `package.json` |
| 旧 electron-rebuild | ^3.2.9 | 需换成 `@electron/rebuild` | 旧 `package.json` |

serialport 10.5.0 对应旧版 Electron，Electron 35 使用较新 V8 引擎。serialport 12.x 是当前稳定版，支持 Node 22 和 Electron 3x 系列。后台 agent 正在确认具体兼容性。

**风险点:** serialport 12.x 的 `@serialport/bindings-cpp` 是 C++ addon，必须针对 Electron 35 的 V8 版本重新编译。这是整个桥接方案的核心技术风险。

### G2: electron-rebuild / @electron/rebuild 流程

**状态: 待处理**

| 项目 | 当前 | 需要 |
|---|---|---|
| rebuild 工具 | 无 | `@electron/rebuild` (非旧 `electron-rebuild`) |
| npmRebuild | `false` (quasar.config.ts:59) | 保持 `false`，手动 rebuild |
| rebuild script | 无 | 添加 `"rebuild": "electron-rebuild -f -w serialport"` |

旧项目用 `electron-rebuild@^3.2.9`（已废弃），新项目应使用 `@electron/rebuild`（官方维护）。旧项目 rebuild 命令为 `electron-rebuild -f -w serialport`。

**验证步骤:**
1. `pnpm -C rewrite add -D @electron/rebuild`
2. `pnpm -C rewrite add serialport`
3. `cd rewrite && npx electron-rebuild -f -w serialport`
4. 检查 `build/Release/bindings.node` 是否生成

### G3: Vite externals 配置

**状态: 待处理 — 当前缺失**

当前 `rewrite/quasar.config.ts` 的 `extendViteConf` 只配了 alias 和 UnoCSS plugin，没有 `rollupOptions.external`。

旧项目配了 4 个 external 包：
```ts
external: ['electron', 'serialport', '@serialport/bindings-cpp', '@serialport/parser-readline', '@serialport/stream']
```

serialport 12.x 的包结构可能与 10.x 不同（合并了 parser 等），需要确认具体需要 external 哪些包。

**需要在 `quasar.config.ts` 的 `extendViteConf` 中添加:**
```ts
viteConf.build ??= {};
viteConf.build.rollupOptions ??= {};
viteConf.build.rollupOptions.external = [
  'electron',
  'serialport',
  // serialport 12.x 的具体子包待安装后确认
];
```

### G4: asarUnpack / package 配置

**状态: 待处理 — 当前配置不完整**

| 项目 | 当前值 | 需要 |
|---|---|---|
| `asar` | `true` | 保持 `true` |
| `asarUnpack` | 无 | 需要添加 |
| `npmRebuild` | `false` | 保持，手动 rebuild |

旧项目直接 `asar: false`（packager 层面），这是最简单的做法但不推荐（全部不压缩）。正确的做法是 `asar: true` + `asarUnpack` 排除 native module。

**需要的配置:**
```ts
asar: true,
asarUnpack: [
  '**/*.node',
  '**/node_modules/serialport/**',
  '**/node_modules/@serialport/**',
],
```

### G5: typed preload bridge shape

**状态: 通过 — 形状已确认**

当前 preload bridge 最小形状：
```ts
const rewriteBridge: RewritePlatformBridge = Object.freeze({
  getBridgeInfo: () => createRewriteBridgeInfo('0.0.0'),
});
```

Bridge implementation design §3 定义了 transport 需要 的 category:
- 发现 (枚举串口)
- 生命周期命令 (连接/断开/清理)
- 字节 I/O (写请求)
- 事件排空 (拉取 main 缓冲事件)
- 背压查询

`RewritePlatformCapability` 当前为 `never`，需要扩展为 transport capability 联合类型。`ConnectionTransportAdapter` port 接口已经覆盖 connect/disconnect/write/cleanup/drainEvents，与 design §4 一致。

**结论: 结构可以承载，无需改 port 接口。**

### G6: main resource handle lifecycle

**状态: 通过 — 需要确认的已列出**

当前 main 是裸 BrowserWindow，无 IPC handler。Design §7 已定义完整的 lifecycle mapping:
- 打开成功 → 已连接 event
- Native close → 已断开 event
- Native error → error event
- App shutdown → 清理 drains all resources

关键确认项:
1. **打开/关闭幂等性** — 对已关闭资源断开应返回 accepted，对不存在的也返回 accepted
2. **错误映射** — resource-unavailable, open-failed, connect-failed, timeout 等 → 已在 design §7.2 列出
3. **App 退出清理** — 必须 驱除 所有 native handles，目前 main 无 `will-quit` handler

### G7: queue/batch/backpressure 参数

**状态: 通过 — 建议先用保守默认值，不跑基准测试**

Design §6 只定义了原则，具体参数需要 基准测试。评估：

| 参数 | 保守默认值 | 理由 |
|---|---|---|
| maxBatchBytes | 4096 | 旧系统 serial 115200 baud ≈ 11520 bytes/s，4KB 约 350ms 批次 |
| maxBatchWindowMs | 50 | 低频数据不超过 50ms 延迟，高频自动触发 字节大小限制 |
| maxQueueDepth | 100 | 约 400KB 内存占用上限（per-connection） |

**建议:** 先用保守默认值实现，后续有 硬件环境 再跑 基准测试 调参。不需要预先跑 基准测试。

### G8: 现有测试不破坏

**状态: 通过**

```
16 files / 153 tests passing
```

---

## 3. 风险清单

| # | 风险 | 严重度 | 可能性 | 缓解措施 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | serialport 12.x + Electron 35 + Node 22 C++ rebuild 失败 | 高 | 中 | 先在当前环境 `npm rebuild` 验证；保留降级到 serialport 10.x 的备选 |
| R2 | serialport 12.x 包结构变化导致 Vite external 不完整 | 中 | 中 | 安装后 `ls node_modules/serialport` 确认子包 |
| R3 | asar 打包后 native .node 文件路径找不到 | 高 | 高 | asarUnpack 必须覆盖 `**/*.node` 和 serialport 目录 |
| R4 | WSL2 环境下 serialport native module 编译/运行问题 | 高 | 高 | WSL2 可能缺少 USB serial 驱动；最终打包在 Linux 原生环境测试 |
| R5 | Quasar + electron-builder 版本兼容性 | 低 | 低 | 当前 electron-builder 24.13.3 支持 asarUnpack |
| R6 | pnpm strict hoisting 导致 serialport 子包路径问题 | 中 | 中 | 可能需要 `.npmrc` 配置 `public-hoist-pattern[]=*serialport*` |

---

## 4. 实现前必须满足的前置条件

### 必须在实现前完成（阻塞）

1. **安装 serialport 并验证 加载**
   ```bash
   pnpm -C rewrite add serialport
   pnpm -C rewrite add -D @electron/rebuild
   cd rewrite && npx electron-rebuild -f -w serialport
   ```
   验证：在 Node REPL 中 `require('serialport')` 能加载且 `SerialPort.list()` 能运行（即使返回空列表）。

2. **配置 Vite externals**
   在 `rewrite/quasar.config.ts` 的 `extendViteConf` 中添加 `rollupOptions.external`。

3. **配置 asarUnpack**
   在 `rewrite/quasar.config.ts` 的 electron.builder 中添加。

4. **验证 dev 模式下 Electron 能加载 serialport**
   最小验证脚本：main 进程中 `require('serialport')` 不报错，`SerialPort.list()` 返回数组（可以为空）。

### 可以在实现中并行处理（非阻塞）

5. 扩展 `RewritePlatformCapability` 类型
6. 扩展 preload 暴露的 transport bridge
7. 添加 main IPC handler

## 5. 下一步建议

**推荐路径：** 先完成 G1-G4 的 native dependency setup 验证，确认环境可行后再进入 bridge implementation。

**具体步骤：**

1. **本轮延续或新开对话：** 安装 serialport + @electron/rebuild，配置 Vite external + asarUnpack，写最小验证脚本确认 dev 模式下能 加载和 `SerialPort.list()`
2. **如果 G1 验证通过：** 进入 bridge implementation，按 bridge implementation design §9.3 的 Phase 1-3 顺序推进
3. **如果 G1 验证失败：** 先解决 native module 问题（可能需要在非 WSL 环境测试，或评估降级方案）

**最小验证脚本建议**（可以写在 `rewrite/src-electron/main/` 下临时文件中）：
```ts
// 验证 serialport 在 Electron main 中能加载
import { SerialPort } from 'serialport';
SerialPort.list().then(ports => {
  console.log('serialport loaded, ports:', ports.length);
}).catch(err => {
  console.error('serialport error:', err.message);
});
```

> AGENT

后台 agent 返回了关键发现。更新报告中的关键结论：

### G1 更新：serialport 版本确认

**serialport 13.0.0**（最新稳定版，不是 12.x）是推荐版本：
- Engine: `node >= 20.0.0`，兼容 Node 22
- `@serialport/bindings-cpp` 是 serialport 的自动依赖，无需单独安装
- **已知问题** ([#2998](https://github.com/serialport/node-serialport/issues/2998))：serialport 13 的 prebuilt binaries 不包含 Electron 35 的 ABI target，**必须**用 `@electron/rebuild` 从源码编译

**@electron/rebuild 4.0.4**（最新版）：
- 要求 Node >= 22.12.0（当前 22.21.1 ✓）
- ESM-only
- 用法：`npx @electron/rebuild`

### 风险清单更新

R1 风险从"中"提升到**高且确认**：serialport 13 不带 Electron 35 prebuilt，必须本地编译 C++ bindings。需要确认当前 WSL2 环境有完整的 C++ 编译工具链（`g++`、`make`、`python3`）。

### Gate checklist

| # | Gate | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | serialport 版本与 Electron 兼容性 | **pass** | serialport 13.0.0 + Node 22 + Electron 35 兼容，需 rebuild（[#2998](https://github.com/serialport/node-serialport/issues/2998) 已确认 workaround） |
| G2 | electron-rebuild 流程 | **pass** | @electron/rebuild 4.0.4 支持 Node >= 22.12.0，当前 22.21.1；C++ 工具链 g++ 13.3.0 + make + python3 齐备 |
| G3 | Vite external 配置 | **block** | 当前 `extendViteConf` 无 `rollupOptions.external`；需添加 serialport + @serialport/bindings-cpp |
| G4 | asarUnpack 配置 | **block** | 当前 `asar: true` 但无 `asarUnpack`；需添加 `**/*.node` + serialport 目录 |
| G5 | typed preload bridge shape | **pass** | `ConnectionTransportAdapter` port 已覆盖 5 个 method + 6 种 event；bridge implementation design §3 已定义 capability category |
| G6 | main resource handle lifecycle | **pass** | Design §7 已定义完整 lifecycle/error mapping；当前 main 是裸 BrowserWindow 可直接扩展 |
| G7 | queue/batch/backpressure 参数 | **pass** | 建议保守默认值：maxBatchBytes=4096, maxBatchWindowMs=50, maxQueueDepth=100；后续 hardware evidence 调参 |
| G8 | 现有测试不破坏 | **pass** | 16 files / 153 tests 全部通过 |

| R1 | serialport 13 prebuilt 不含 Electron 35 ABI，必须源码编译 | 高 | C++ 工具链已确认齐备；@electron/rebuild 4.0.4 可用 |
| R2 | WSL2 下 serialport 能加载但无法枚举真实串口（无 USB 驱动） | 高 | 开发态可用 virtual serial port 或 mock 验证加载；最终硬件测试在 Linux 原生环境 |
